LedgerIssue2297Tests

Base: red on base: the test measures something new PR: green · 1 passed passed

Test code
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class LedgerIssue2297Tests
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedTypesAreDecoratedWithExcludeFromCodeCoverage()
    {
        string source = """
using System;
using MessagePack;

namespace TestProject
{
    public enum SampleEnum
    {
        First,
        Second,
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class IntKeyedObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public SampleEnum Kind { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public class StringKeyedObject
    {
        public string Name { get; set; }
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(UnionImplA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(UnionImplB))]
    public interface ISampleUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplA : ISampleUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplB : ISampleUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string Text { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class GenericObject<T>
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public T Value { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class RootHolder
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public IntKeyedObject IntObj { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public StringKeyedObject StringObj { get; set; }

        [Key(2)]
        public ISampleUnion UnionObj { get; set; }

        [Key(3)]
        public GenericObject<int> GenericObj { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> references = net80Refs.AddRange(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        SyntaxTree inputTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest));
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { inputTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(output.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        List<SyntaxTree> generatedTrees = output.SyntaxTrees
            .Where(t => !compilation.SyntaxTrees.Contains(t))
            .ToList();
        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedTrees);

        List<string> typesMissingAttribute = new List<string>();
        int checkedTypeCount = 0;

        foreach (SyntaxTree tree in generatedTrees)
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(tree);
            SyntaxNode root = await tree.GetRootAsync(CancellationToken.None);

            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                INamedTypeSymbol? typeSymbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl);
                if (typeSymbol is null)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (typeSymbol.ContainingType is not null)
                {
                    continue;
                }

                if (typeSymbol.TypeKind is not (TypeKind.Class or TypeKind.Struct))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                checkedTypeCount++;
                bool hasExcludeAttribute = typeSymbol.GetAttributes().Any(attr =>
                    SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));

                if (!hasExcludeAttribute)
                {
                    typesMissingAttribute.Add(typeSymbol.ToDisplayString());
                }
            }
        }

        Assert.True(checkedTypeCount > 0, "Expected at least one generated class or struct.");
        Assert.Empty(typesMissingAttribute);
    }
}

Prosecutor 1: When the generated project targets netstandard2.0 or netframework, does the generated resolver omit the ExcludeFromCodeCoverage attribute and still compile without it?

The change guards the attribute with #if !NETSTANDARD2_0 && !NETFRAMEWORK, but the change test and invariance check run under a single target framework and never verify the excluded branches or compilation under those TFMs.

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Emit;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    private const string TestSource = """
using MessagePack;
using MessagePack.Resolvers;

namespace TestNamespace
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class SampleObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }

    [CompositeResolver(typeof(BuiltinResolver), typeof(StandardResolver))]
    public partial class MyCompositeResolver
    {
    }
}
""";

    [Fact]
    public async Task CompositeResolver_NetStandard20_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageAndCompiles()
    {
        var (runResult, outputCompilation) = await RunGeneratorAsync("NETSTANDARD2_0");
        AssertCompositeResolverGeneratedAndCompiled(runResult, outputCompilation, expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage: false);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task CompositeResolver_NetFramework_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageAndCompiles()
    {
        var (runResult, outputCompilation) = await RunGeneratorAsync("NETFRAMEWORK");
        AssertCompositeResolverGeneratedAndCompiled(runResult, outputCompilation, expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage: false);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task CompositeResolver_Net80_IncludesExcludeFromCodeCoverageAndCompiles()
    {
        var (runResult, outputCompilation) = await RunGeneratorAsync();
        AssertCompositeResolverGeneratedAndCompiled(runResult, outputCompilation, expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage: true);
    }

    private static async Task<(GeneratorDriverRunResult RunResult, Compilation OutputCompilation)> RunGeneratorAsync(params string[] preprocessorSymbols)
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        MetadataReference[] references = frameworkRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack)).ToArray();

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: preprocessorSymbols);
        SyntaxTree syntaxTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(TestSource, parseOptions);

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "CompositeResolverTestAssembly",
            new[] { syntaxTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> inputErrors = compilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error).ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.True(inputErrors.IsEmpty, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, inputErrors));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(
            new[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() },
            parseOptions: parseOptions);
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorErrors = generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error).ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.True(generatorErrors.IsEmpty, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, generatorErrors));

        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));

        return (runResult, outputCompilation);
    }

    private static void AssertCompositeResolverGeneratedAndCompiled(
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult,
        Compilation outputCompilation,
        bool expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage)
    {
        GeneratedSourceResult[] generatedSources = runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources).ToArray();
        GeneratedSourceResult compositeResolverSource = generatedSources.FirstOrDefault(s =>
            s.HintName.Contains("MyCompositeResolver", StringComparison.Ordinal) ||
            s.SourceText.ToString().Contains("partial class MyCompositeResolver", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        Assert.NotNull(compositeResolverSource.SyntaxTree);

        string generatedText = compositeResolverSource.SourceText.ToString();
        Assert.Contains("#if !NETSTANDARD2_0 && !NETFRAMEWORK", generatedText, StringComparison.Ordinal);
        Assert.Contains("[global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverage]", generatedText, StringComparison.Ordinal);

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> compilationErrors = outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error).ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.True(compilationErrors.IsEmpty, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, compilationErrors));

        using MemoryStream peStream = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult emitResult = outputCompilation.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));

        INamedTypeSymbol? resolverType = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.MyCompositeResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(resolverType);
        Assert.Equal(expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage, HasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute(resolverType!));

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterCacheType = resolverType!.GetTypeMembers("FormatterCache").SingleOrDefault();
        Assert.NotNull(formatterCacheType);
        Assert.Equal(expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage, HasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute(formatterCacheType!));
    }

    private static bool HasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute(ISymbol symbol)
    {
        return symbol.GetAttributes().Any(a =>
            string.Equals(a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString(), "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute", StringComparison.Ordinal));
    }
}

Defender: Ledger_2297_Objection2 · PR green · 1 passed · mutant green · 1 passed · no valid test

Defender's test
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task Union_Enum_StringKey_Formatters_And_Resolvers_Have_ExcludeFromCodeCoverage()
    {
        string source = """
            using MessagePack;

            namespace TestNamespace
            {
                public enum MyEnum
                {
                    First = 0,
                    Second = 1,
                }

                [Union(0, typeof(UnionImplA))]
                [Union(1, typeof(UnionImplB))]
                public interface IMyUnion
                {
                }

                [MessagePackObject]
                public class UnionImplA : IMyUnion
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public int Value { get; set; }
                }

                [MessagePackObject]
                public class UnionImplB : IMyUnion
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public string Name { get; set; }
                }

                [MessagePackObject(true)]
                public class StringKeyObject
                {
                    public int Id { get; set; }
                    public MyEnum Status { get; set; }
                    public IMyUnion Payload { get; set; }
                }

                [GeneratedMessagePackResolver]
                public partial class CustomCompositeResolver
                {
                }
            }
            """;

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = net80Refs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.NotEmpty(runResult.Results);

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> compileErrors = outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics()
            .Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error)
            .ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.Empty(compileErrors);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        bool HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(INamedTypeSymbol symbol) =>
            symbol.GetAttributes().Any(a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));

        var generatedTypes = new HashSet<INamedTypeSymbol>(SymbolEqualityComparer.Default);
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources))
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(generatedSource.SyntaxTree);
            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetRoot().DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl) is INamedTypeSymbol symbol)
                {
                    generatedTypes.Add(symbol);
                }
            }
        }

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> formatterTypes = generatedTypes
            .Where(t => t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => i.Name == "IMessagePackFormatter"))
            .ToList();

        INamedTypeSymbol? unionSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.IMyUnion");
        INamedTypeSymbol? enumSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.MyEnum");
        INamedTypeSymbol? stringKeySymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.StringKeyObject");
        Assert.NotNull(unionSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(enumSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(stringKeySymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? unionFormatter = formatterTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
            t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => i.Name == "IMessagePackFormatter" && i.TypeArguments.Length == 1 && SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.TypeArguments[0], unionSymbol)));
        INamedTypeSymbol? enumFormatter = formatterTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
            t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => i.Name == "IMessagePackFormatter" && i.TypeArguments.Length == 1 && SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.TypeArguments[0], enumSymbol)));
        INamedTypeSymbol? stringKeyFormatter = formatterTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
            t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => i.Name == "IMessagePackFormatter" && i.TypeArguments.Length == 1 && SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.TypeArguments[0], stringKeySymbol)));

        Assert.NotNull(unionFormatter);
        Assert.NotNull(enumFormatter);
        Assert.NotNull(stringKeyFormatter);

        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol formatterType in formatterTypes)
        {
            Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(formatterType), $"Expected {formatterType.ToDisplayString()} to have [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
        }

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> resolverTypes = generatedTypes
            .Where(t => t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => i.Name == "IFormatterResolver"))
            .ToList();
        Assert.NotEmpty(resolverTypes);

        INamedTypeSymbol? customResolverSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.CustomCompositeResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(customResolverSymbol);
        Assert.Contains(customResolverSymbol!, resolverTypes, SymbolEqualityComparer.Default);

        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol resolverType in resolverTypes)
        {
            Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolverType), $"Expected {resolverType.ToDisplayString()} to have [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
            INamedTypeSymbol? formatterCache = resolverType.GetTypeMembers("FormatterCache").FirstOrDefault();
            if (formatterCache is not null)
            {
                Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(formatterCache), $"Expected {formatterCache.ToDisplayString()} to have [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Emit;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task NestedCompositeResolver_EmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageOnOuterAndFormatterCache()
    {
        string source = """
            #nullable enable
            using MessagePack;
            using MessagePack.Resolvers;

            namespace TempProject
            {
                public partial class Outer
                {
                    [CompositeResolver(typeof(BuiltinResolver))]
                    public partial class NestedCompositeResolver
                    {
                    }
                }
            }
            """;

        await VerifyCompositeResolverAsync(source, "NestedCompositeResolver", "TempProject.Outer+NestedCompositeResolver");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ClosedGenericCompositeResolver_EmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageOnOuterAndFormatterCache()
    {
        string source = """
            #nullable enable
            using MessagePack;
            using MessagePack.Formatters;
            using MessagePack.Resolvers;

            namespace TempProject
            {
                public class MyGenericResolver<T> : IFormatterResolver
                {
                    public static readonly MyGenericResolver<T> Instance = new MyGenericResolver<T>();

                    public MyGenericResolver()
                    {
                    }

                    public IMessagePackFormatter<U>? GetFormatter<U>() => null;
                }

                [CompositeResolver(typeof(MyGenericResolver<int>), typeof(BuiltinResolver))]
                public partial class ClosedGenericCompositeResolver
                {
                }
            }
            """;

        await VerifyCompositeResolverAsync(source, "ClosedGenericCompositeResolver", "TempProject.ClosedGenericCompositeResolver");
    }

    private static async Task VerifyCompositeResolverAsync(string source, string resolverSimpleName, string resolverMetadataName)
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        MetadataReference[] references = net80Refs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack)).ToArray();

        SyntaxTree syntaxTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest));
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { syntaxTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary, nullableContextOptions: NullableContextOptions.Enable));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePack.SourceGenerator.MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.DoesNotContain(generatorDiagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));

        GeneratedSourceResult[] matchingSources = runResult.Results
            .SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources)
            .Where(s =>
            {
                string text = s.SourceText.ToString();
                return text.Contains($"partial class {resolverSimpleName}") && text.Contains("IFormatterResolver");
            })
            .ToArray();

        Assert.Single(matchingSources);
        string generatedCode = matchingSources[0].SourceText.ToString();
        Assert.Contains("[global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverage]", generatedCode);

        Diagnostic[] outputErrors = output.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error).ToArray();
        Assert.Empty(outputErrors);

        using MemoryStream peStream = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult emitResult = output.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));

        INamedTypeSymbol? resolverSymbol = output.GetTypeByMetadataName(resolverMetadataName);
        Assert.NotNull(resolverSymbol);
        Assert.Contains(
            resolverSymbol!.GetAttributes(),
            a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");

        INamedTypeSymbol? cacheSymbol = resolverSymbol.GetTypeMembers("FormatterCache").SingleOrDefault();
        Assert.NotNull(cacheSymbol);
        Assert.Contains(
            cacheSymbol!.GetAttributes(),
            a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }
}

Prosecutor · nvidia/nemotron-3.5-super-vl-120b-a12b

Prosecutor 1: When the source generator runs with NETSTANDARD2_0 or NETFRAMEWORK defined, is ExcludeFromCodeCoverage absent from the generated resolver and FormatterCache<T>, and when those symbols are undefined, is the attribute present on both?

The change test and invariance check execute under one fixed target framework. They do not verify the #if guards, so they cannot confirm the attribute is correctly suppressed on older frameworks where it may be unavailable, or correctly emitted on modern frameworks.

Defender · gemini-4-hydrogen · may answer only with a test

Defender: Ledger_2297_Objection1 · PR green · 3 passed · mutant does not compile · no valid test

Defender's test
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    private const string ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttributeName = "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute";

    [Fact]
    public async Task ExcludeFromCodeCoverage_PresentWhenSymbolsUndefined()
    {
        Compilation output = await RunGeneratorAsync();
        INamedTypeSymbol? resolver = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.MyCompositeResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(resolver);
        INamedTypeSymbol formatterCache = Assert.Single(resolver.GetTypeMembers("FormatterCache"));

        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolver), "Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute on generated resolver.");
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(formatterCache), "Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute on generated FormatterCache<T>.");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ExcludeFromCodeCoverage_AbsentWhenNetStandard20Defined()
    {
        Compilation output = await RunGeneratorAsync("NETSTANDARD2_0");
        INamedTypeSymbol? resolver = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.MyCompositeResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(resolver);
        INamedTypeSymbol formatterCache = Assert.Single(resolver.GetTypeMembers("FormatterCache"));

        Assert.False(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolver), "Expected no ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute on generated resolver when NETSTANDARD2_0 is defined.");
        Assert.False(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(formatterCache), "Expected no ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute on generated FormatterCache<T> when NETSTANDARD2_0 is defined.");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ExcludeFromCodeCoverage_AbsentWhenNetFrameworkDefined()
    {
        Compilation output = await RunGeneratorAsync("NETFRAMEWORK");
        INamedTypeSymbol? resolver = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.MyCompositeResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(resolver);
        INamedTypeSymbol formatterCache = Assert.Single(resolver.GetTypeMembers("FormatterCache"));

        Assert.False(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolver), "Expected no ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute on generated resolver when NETFRAMEWORK is defined.");
        Assert.False(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(formatterCache), "Expected no ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute on generated FormatterCache<T> when NETFRAMEWORK is defined.");
    }

    private static bool HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(ISymbol symbol) =>
        symbol.GetAttributes().Any(a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttributeName);

    private static async Task<Compilation> RunGeneratorAsync(params string[] preprocessorSymbols)
    {
        string source = """
            using MessagePack;

            namespace TempProject
            {
                [MessagePackObject]
                public class MyObject
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public int Value { get; set; }
                }

                [GeneratedMessagePackResolver]
                public partial class MyCompositeResolver
                {
                }
            }
            """;

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> references = (await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None))
            .AddRange(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: preprocessorSymbols);
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(
            new[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() },
            parseOptions: parseOptions);
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);

        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();
        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.NotEmpty(runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources));
        Assert.Empty(output.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        return output;
    }

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.Linq;
using System.Reflection;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;
using Xunit;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task CompositeResolverTemplate_AppliesExcludeFromCodeCoverageToAllGeneratedTypes()
    {
        Assembly[] candidateAssemblies = new[]
        {
            typeof(MessagePackObjectAttribute).Assembly,
            typeof(MessagePackSerializer).Assembly,
        }.Distinct().ToArray();

        Type? attrType = candidateAssemblies
            .SelectMany(a => a.GetExportedTypes())
            .FirstOrDefault(t => typeof(Attribute).IsAssignableFrom(t)
                && t.Name.Contains("Composite", StringComparison.Ordinal)
                && t.Name.Contains("Resolver", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        if (attrType is null)
        {
            attrType = candidateAssemblies
                .SelectMany(a => a.GetExportedTypes())
                .FirstOrDefault(t => typeof(Attribute).IsAssignableFrom(t)
                    && t.Name == "GeneratedMessagePackResolverAttribute");
        }

        Assert.NotNull(attrType);

        string attrFullName = $"global::{attrType!.FullName!.Replace('+', '.')}";
        string resolverTypeArg = "typeof(global::MessagePack.Resolvers.StandardResolver)";
        string resolverArrayArg = $"new global::System.Type[] {{ {resolverTypeArg} }}";

        ConstructorInfo? typeArrayCtor = attrType.GetConstructors().FirstOrDefault(c =>
        {
            ParameterInfo[] p = c.GetParameters();
            return p.Length >= 1 && p[0].ParameterType == typeof(Type[]) && p.Skip(1).All(x => x.IsOptional);
        });

        ConstructorInfo? singleTypeCtor = attrType.GetConstructors().FirstOrDefault(c =>
        {
            ParameterInfo[] p = c.GetParameters();
            return p.Length >= 1 && p.All(x => x.ParameterType == typeof(Type));
        });

        ConstructorInfo? parameterlessCtor = attrType.GetConstructor(Type.EmptyTypes);

        string attrUsage;
        if (typeArrayCtor is not null)
        {
            attrUsage = $"[{attrFullName}({resolverArrayArg})]";
        }
        else if (parameterlessCtor is not null)
        {
            PropertyInfo? typeArrayProp = attrType.GetProperties(BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Instance)
                .FirstOrDefault(p => p.CanWrite && p.PropertyType == typeof(Type[]));
            if (typeArrayProp is not null)
            {
                attrUsage = $"[{attrFullName}({typeArrayProp.Name} = {resolverArrayArg})]";
            }
            else
            {
                attrUsage = $"[{attrFullName}]";
            }
        }
        else if (singleTypeCtor is not null)
        {
            string args = string.Join(", ", Enumerable.Repeat(resolverTypeArg, singleTypeCtor.GetParameters().Length));
            attrUsage = $"[{attrFullName}({args})]";
        }
        else
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Unsupported constructors on {attrType.FullName}");
        }

        string testSource = $$"""
using MessagePack;

namespace TestProject
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class SampleMessagePackObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }

    {{attrUsage}}
    partial class TestCompositeResolver
    {
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = net80Refs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestCompositeResolverAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(testSource, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r =>
        {
            Assert.Null(r.Exception);
            Assert.Empty(r.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        });

        Diagnostic[] compileErrors = outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics()
            .Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error)
            .ToArray();
        Assert.Empty(compileErrors);

        GeneratedSourceResult? resolverSource = null;
        foreach (GeneratorRunResult result in runResult.Results)
        {
            foreach (GeneratedSourceResult source in result.GeneratedSources)
            {
                bool hasResolverDecl = source.SyntaxTree.GetRoot()
                    .DescendantNodes()
                    .OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>()
                    .Any(t => t.Identifier.Text == "TestCompositeResolver");
                if (hasResolverDecl)
                {
                    resolverSource = source;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (resolverSource.HasValue)
            {
                break;
            }
        }

        Assert.True(resolverSource.HasValue, "Expected generated source for TestCompositeResolver.");

        SyntaxTree generatedTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(resolverSource.Value.SourceText, parseOptions);
        List<BaseTypeDeclarationSyntax> generatedTypes = generatedTree.GetRoot()
            .DescendantNodes()
            .OfType<BaseTypeDeclarationSyntax>()
            .ToList();

        Assert.True(generatedTypes.Count >= 2, $"Expected at least 2 generated type declarations (resolver and FormatterCache<T>), found {generatedTypes.Count}.");

        foreach (BaseTypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in generatedTypes)
        {
            bool hasExcludeAttribute = typeDecl.AttributeLists
                .SelectMany(list => list.Attributes)
                .Any(attr => attr.Name.ToString().Contains("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal));

            Assert.True(
                hasExcludeAttribute,
                $"Generated type '{typeDecl.Identifier.Text}' in '{resolverSource.Value.HintName}' is missing [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
        }

        INamedTypeSymbol? resolverSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestProject.TestCompositeResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(resolverSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeAttrSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeAttrSymbol);

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> allResolverTypes = new();
        void CollectTypes(INamedTypeSymbol symbol)
        {
            allResolverTypes.Add(symbol);
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in symbol.GetTypeMembers())
            {
                CollectTypes(nested);
            }
        }

        CollectTypes(resolverSymbol!);
        Assert.True(allResolverTypes.Count >= 2, $"Expected resolver and at least one nested type, found {allResolverTypes.Count}.");

        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol typeSymbol in allResolverTypes)
        {
            bool hasExcludeAttr = typeSymbol.GetAttributes().Any(a =>
                SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeAttrSymbol));

            Assert.True(
                hasExcludeAttr,
                $"Compiled type symbol '{typeSymbol.ToDisplayString()}' is missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;

using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task AllGeneratedTemplatesEmitExcludeFromCodeCoverage()
    {
        string source = @"
using MessagePack;

namespace TempProject
{
    [GeneratedMessagePackResolver]
    public partial class MyCompositeResolver
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class IntKeyObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public MyEnum Status { get; set; }

        [Key(2)]
        public IMyUnion UnionValue { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public class StringKeyObject
    {
        public string Name { get; set; }
        public int Count { get; set; }
    }

    public enum MyEnum
    {
        None = 0,
        Active = 1,
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(UnionImplA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(UnionImplB))]
    public interface IMyUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplA : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int A { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplB : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string B { get; set; }
    }
}
";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> msgPackRefs = ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack);
        MetadataReference[] references = net80Refs.Concat(msgPackRefs).ToArray();

        SyntaxTree syntaxTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest));
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { syntaxTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(runResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        foreach (GeneratorRunResult result in runResult.Results)
        {
            Assert.Null(result.Exception);
        }

        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeAttrSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeAttrSymbol);

        bool HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(ISymbol symbol) =>
            symbol.GetAttributes().Any(a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeAttrSymbol));

        var generatedTypeSymbols = new List<INamedTypeSymbol>();
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources))
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(generatedSource.SyntaxTree);
            SyntaxNode root = await generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetRootAsync(CancellationToken.None);
            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                INamedTypeSymbol? typeSymbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl);
                Assert.NotNull(typeSymbol);
                generatedTypeSymbols.Add(typeSymbol);
                Assert.True(
                    HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(typeSymbol),
                    $"Generated type '{typeSymbol.ToDisplayString()}' in '{generatedSource.HintName}' must have [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
            }
        }

        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedTypeSymbols);

        INamedTypeSymbol? resolverSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.MyCompositeResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(resolverSymbol);
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolverSymbol), "Generated composite resolver partial class must have [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterCacheSymbol = resolverSymbol.GetTypeMembers("FormatterCache").SingleOrDefault();
        Assert.NotNull(formatterCacheSymbol);
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(formatterCacheSymbol), "Generated FormatterCache<T> must have [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);

        string[] targetTypeNames =
        {
            "TempProject.IntKeyObject",
            "TempProject.StringKeyObject",
            "TempProject.MyEnum",
            "TempProject.IMyUnion",
        };

        foreach (string targetTypeName in targetTypeNames)
        {
            INamedTypeSymbol? targetType = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName(targetTypeName);
            Assert.NotNull(targetType);

            INamedTypeSymbol expectedInterface = formatterInterface.Construct(targetType);
            INamedTypeSymbol? generatedFormatter = generatedTypeSymbols
                .FirstOrDefault(t => t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, expectedInterface)));

            Assert.NotNull(generatedFormatter);
            Assert.True(
                HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(generatedFormatter),
                $"Generated formatter '{generatedFormatter.ToDisplayString()}' for '{targetTypeName}' must have [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task AllGeneratedFormattersAndResolver_EmitExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TempProject
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class MyObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public MyEnum Status { get; set; }

        [Key(2)]
        public MyStringKeyObject Nested { get; set; }

        [Key(3)]
        public IMyUnion UnionValue { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public class MyStringKeyObject
    {
        public string Name { get; set; }
    }

    public enum MyEnum
    {
        A,
        B,
        C,
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(UnionImplA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(UnionImplB))]
    public interface IMyUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplA : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplB : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string Text { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = frameworkRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        Assert.Empty(compilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);

        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        List<GeneratedSourceResult> generatedSources = runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources).ToList();
        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedSources);

        INamedTypeSymbol excludeAttrSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute")!;
        Assert.NotNull(excludeAttrSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol formatterInterfaceSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1")!;
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterfaceSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol resolverInterfaceSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.IFormatterResolver")!;
        Assert.NotNull(resolverInterfaceSymbol);

        var generatedTypes = new List<INamedTypeSymbol>();
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in generatedSources)
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(generatedSource.SyntaxTree);
            SyntaxNode root = await generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetRootAsync();
            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl) is INamedTypeSymbol typeSymbol)
                {
                    generatedTypes.Add(typeSymbol);
                }
            }
        }

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> distinctGeneratedTypes = generatedTypes
            .Distinct<INamedTypeSymbol>(SymbolEqualityComparer.Default)
            .ToList();

        INamedTypeSymbol myObjectSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.MyObject")!;
        INamedTypeSymbol myStringKeyObjectSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.MyStringKeyObject")!;
        INamedTypeSymbol myEnumSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.MyEnum")!;
        INamedTypeSymbol myUnionSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.IMyUnion")!;

        Assert.NotNull(myObjectSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(myStringKeyObjectSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(myEnumSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(myUnionSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol FindFormatterFor(INamedTypeSymbol targetType) =>
            distinctGeneratedTypes.Single(t =>
                t.AllInterfaces.Any(i =>
                    SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.OriginalDefinition, formatterInterfaceSymbol) &&
                    SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.TypeArguments[0], targetType)));

        INamedTypeSymbol myObjectFormatter = FindFormatterFor(myObjectSymbol);
        INamedTypeSymbol myStringKeyObjectFormatter = FindFormatterFor(myStringKeyObjectSymbol);
        INamedTypeSymbol myEnumFormatter = FindFormatterFor(myEnumSymbol);
        INamedTypeSymbol myUnionFormatter = FindFormatterFor(myUnionSymbol);

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> resolvers = distinctGeneratedTypes
            .Where(t => t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, resolverInterfaceSymbol)))
            .ToList();

        Assert.NotEmpty(resolvers);

        bool HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(INamedTypeSymbol type) =>
            type.GetAttributes().Any(a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeAttrSymbol));

        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(myObjectFormatter), $"Missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {myObjectFormatter.ToDisplayString()}");
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(myStringKeyObjectFormatter), $"Missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {myStringKeyObjectFormatter.ToDisplayString()}");
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(myEnumFormatter), $"Missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {myEnumFormatter.ToDisplayString()}");
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(myUnionFormatter), $"Missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {myUnionFormatter.ToDisplayString()}");
        Assert.All(resolvers, resolver =>
            Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolver), $"Missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {resolver.ToDisplayString()}"));
    }
}

    {
        [Key(0)]
        public MyEnum Value { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

    [Fact]
    public async Task EnumFormatter_DefaultTarget_IncludesExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        INamedTypeSymbol enumFormatterType = await GenerateAndCompileEnumFormatterAsync();
        Assert.Contains(
            enumFormatterType.GetAttributes(),
            a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task EnumFormatter_NetStandard20_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        INamedTypeSymbol enumFormatterType = await GenerateAndCompileEnumFormatterAsync("NETSTANDARD2_0");
        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            enumFormatterType.GetAttributes(),
            a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task EnumFormatter_NetFramework_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        INamedTypeSymbol enumFormatterType = await GenerateAndCompileEnumFormatterAsync("NETFRAMEWORK");
        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            enumFormatterType.GetAttributes(),
            a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    private static async Task<INamedTypeSymbol> GenerateAndCompileEnumFormatterAsync(params string[] preprocessorSymbols)
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkReferences = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = frameworkReferences.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: preprocessorSymbols);
        SyntaxTree syntaxTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(TestSource, parseOptions);

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { syntaxTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(
            new[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() },
            parseOptions: parseOptions);

        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.DoesNotContain(generatorDiagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r =>
        {
            Assert.Null(r.Exception);
            Assert.DoesNotContain(r.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);
        });

        string? enumFormatterSource = runResult.Results
            .SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources)
            .Select(s => s.SourceText.ToString())
            .FirstOrDefault(text => text.Contains("IMessagePackFormatter<") && text.Contains("TestNamespace.MyEnum"));

        Assert.NotNull(enumFormatterSource);
        Assert.Contains("#if !NETSTANDARD2_0 && !NETFRAMEWORK", enumFormatterSource);
        Assert.Contains("[global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverage]", enumFormatterSource);

        using MemoryStream peStream = new MemoryStream();
        var emitResult = output.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));

        INamedTypeSymbol? enumSymbol = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.MyEnum");
        Assert.NotNull(enumSymbol);
        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);
        INamedTypeSymbol constructedInterface = formatterInterface.Construct(enumSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? enumFormatterType = GetAllTypes(output.Assembly.GlobalNamespace)
            .FirstOrDefault(t => t.AllInterfaces.Contains(constructedInterface, SymbolEqualityComparer.Default));
        Assert.NotNull(enumFormatterType);

        return enumFormatterType;
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetAllTypes(INamespaceSymbol ns)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in ns.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return type;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in GetNestedTypes(type))
            {
                yield return nested;
            }
        }

        foreach (INamespaceSymbol childNs in ns.GetNamespaceMembers())
        {
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in GetAllTypes(childNs))
            {
                yield return type;
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetNestedTypes(INamedTypeSymbol type)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in type.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return nested;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol deep in GetNestedTypes(nested))
            {
                yield return deep;
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task EnumFromReferencedAssembly_GeneratedFormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> netRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);

        string externalEnumSource = """
namespace ExternalAssembly
{
    public enum ReferencedEnum
    {
        First = 0,
        Second = 1,
    }
}
""";

        CSharpCompilation externalCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ExternalEnumAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(externalEnumSource, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            netRefs,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        using var peStream = new MemoryStream();
        var emitResult = externalCompilation.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));

        MetadataReference externalRef = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(peStream.ToArray());

        string consumerSource = """
using MessagePack;
using ExternalAssembly;

namespace ConsumerAssembly
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class MyObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public ReferencedEnum Value { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> consumerRefs = netRefs
            .Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack))
            .Append(externalRef);

        CSharpCompilation consumerCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ConsumerAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(consumerSource, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            consumerRefs,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(consumerCompilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics);
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> outputDiagnostics = output.GetDiagnostics();
        Assert.DoesNotContain(outputDiagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);

        INamedTypeSymbol? enumSymbol = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("ExternalAssembly.ReferencedEnum");
        Assert.NotNull(enumSymbol);
        Assert.Empty(enumSymbol!.DeclaringSyntaxReferences);

        INamedTypeSymbol expectedInterface = formatterInterface!.Construct(enumSymbol);
        List<INamedTypeSymbol> matchingFormatters = GetAllTypes(output.Assembly.GlobalNamespace)
            .Where(t => t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, expectedInterface)))
            .ToList();

        INamedTypeSymbol generatedFormatter = Assert.Single(matchingFormatters);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        Assert.Contains(
            generatedFormatter.GetAttributes(),
            attr => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));

        GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource = Assert.Single(
            runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources),
            s => s.SourceText.ToString().Contains("IMessagePackFormatter<global::ExternalAssembly.ReferencedEnum>"));

        Assert.Contains("[global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverage]", generatedSource.SourceText.ToString());
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetAllTypes(INamespaceSymbol ns)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in ns.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return type;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in GetNestedTypes(type))
            {
                yield return nested;
            }
        }

        foreach (INamespaceSymbol childNs in ns.GetNamespaceMembers())
        {
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in GetAllTypes(childNs))
            {
                yield return type;
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetNestedTypes(INamedTypeSymbol type)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in type.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return nested;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol deeper in GetNestedTypes(nested))
            {
                yield return deeper;
            }
        }
    }
}

#nullable enable
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Emit;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task ReferencedAssemblyEnumFormatter_HasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net8Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);

        string externalSource = """
            namespace ExternalNamespace
            {
                public enum ExternalEnum
                {
                    First = 0,
                    Second = 1,
                }
            }
            """;

        CSharpCompilation externalCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ExternalEnumAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(externalSource, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            net8Refs,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        using MemoryStream externalPeStream = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult emitResult = externalCompilation.Emit(externalPeStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));
        MetadataReference externalReference = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(externalPeStream.ToArray());

        string consumerSource = """
            using MessagePack;
            using ExternalNamespace;

            namespace ConsumerNamespace
            {
                [MessagePackObject]
                public class MyMessagePackObject
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public ExternalEnum EnumValue { get; set; }
                }
            }
            """;

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> references = net8Refs
            .AddRange(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack))
            .Add(externalReference);

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ConsumerAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(consumerSource, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePack.SourceGenerator.MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        INamedTypeSymbol? externalEnumSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("ExternalNamespace.ExternalEnum");
        Assert.NotNull(externalEnumSymbol);
        Assert.Empty(externalEnumSymbol!.DeclaringSyntaxReferences);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);

        INamedTypeSymbol expectedFormatterInterface = formatterInterface!.Construct(externalEnumSymbol);

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> enumFormatters = GetAllNamedTypes(outputCompilation.Assembly.GlobalNamespace)
            .Where(t => t.AllInterfaces.Contains(expectedFormatterInterface, SymbolEqualityComparer.Default))
            .ToList();

        INamedTypeSymbol enumFormatter = Assert.Single(enumFormatters);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        Assert.Contains(
            enumFormatter.GetAttributes(),
            attr => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetAllNamedTypes(INamespaceSymbol root)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in root.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return type;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in GetNestedTypes(type))
            {
                yield return nested;
            }
        }

        foreach (INamespaceSymbol ns in root.GetNamespaceMembers())
        {
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in GetAllNamedTypes(ns))
            {
                yield return type;
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetNestedTypes(INamedTypeSymbol type)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in type.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return nested;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol deeper in GetNestedTypes(nested))
            {
                yield return deeper;
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;
using Xunit;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task UnionAndEnumGeneratedFormatters_HaveExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TempProject
{
    public enum MyEnum
    {
        First = 0,
        Second = 1,
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(SubTypeA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(SubTypeB))]
    public interface IMyUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class SubTypeA : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public MyEnum EnumValue { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class SubTypeB : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int NumberValue { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = net80Refs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(output.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        INamedTypeSymbol? unionType = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.IMyUnion");
        Assert.NotNull(unionType);

        INamedTypeSymbol? enumType = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.MyEnum");
        Assert.NotNull(enumType);

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> generatedFormatterTypes = new();
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources))
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(generatedSource.SyntaxTree);
            SyntaxNode root = await generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetRootAsync();
            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl) is INamedTypeSymbol typeSymbol &&
                    typeSymbol.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.OriginalDefinition, formatterInterface)))
                {
                    generatedFormatterTypes.Add(typeSymbol);
                }
            }
        }

        INamedTypeSymbol? unionFormatter = generatedFormatterTypes.SingleOrDefault(t =>
            t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.OriginalDefinition, formatterInterface)
                && SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.TypeArguments[0], unionType)));
        Assert.NotNull(unionFormatter);
        Assert.Contains(
            unionFormatter!.GetAttributes(),
            a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));

        INamedTypeSymbol? enumFormatter = generatedFormatterTypes.SingleOrDefault(t =>
            t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.OriginalDefinition, formatterInterface)
                && SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.TypeArguments[0], enumType)));
        Assert.NotNull(enumFormatter);
        Assert.Contains(
            enumFormatter!.GetAttributes(),
            a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));
    }
}

Prosecutor · nvidia/nemotron-3.5-super-vl-120b-a12b

Prosecutor 3: For an enum type nested inside a generic class (for example Outer<T>.Inner), does the generated formatter class include the ExcludeFromCodeCoverage attribute?

The invariance check covers 21 general cases but may not include enums nested in generic types, where qualified-name generation and namespace/nesting emission differ.

Defender · gemini-4-hydrogen · may answer only with a test

Defender: Ledger_2297_Objection3 · PR green · 1 passed · mutant does not compile · no valid test

Defender's test
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task EnumNestedInGenericClass_FormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TempProject
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class MyObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public Outer<int>.Inner Value { get; set; }
    }

    public class Outer<T>
    {
        public enum Inner
        {
            A,
            B,
        }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80References = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        var references = new List<MetadataReference>(net80References);
        references.AddRange(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.All(runResult.Results, result => Assert.Null(result.Exception));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(generatorDiagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics(), d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);

        INamedTypeSymbol? outerOpenSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TempProject.Outer`1");
        Assert.NotNull(outerOpenSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? innerOpenSymbol = outerOpenSymbol.GetTypeMembers("Inner").SingleOrDefault();
        Assert.NotNull(innerOpenSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterfaceOpenSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterfaceOpenSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute);

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> formatterTypes = GetAllTypes(outputCompilation.Assembly.GlobalNamespace)
            .Where(type => type.AllInterfaces.Any(iface =>
                SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(iface.OriginalDefinition, formatterInterfaceOpenSymbol) &&
                iface.TypeArguments.Length == 1 &&
                SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(iface.TypeArguments[0].OriginalDefinition, innerOpenSymbol)))
            .ToList();

        Assert.NotEmpty(formatterTypes);

        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol formatterType in formatterTypes)
        {
            bool hasExcludeAttribute = formatterType.GetAttributes().Any(
                attr => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute));
            Assert.True(hasExcludeAttribute, $"Expected {formatterType.ToDisplayString()} to be decorated with ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetAllTypes(INamespaceSymbol ns)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in ns.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return type;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in GetNestedTypes(type))
            {
                yield return nested;
            }
        }

        foreach (INamespaceSymbol childNs in ns.GetNamespaceMembers())
        {
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in GetAllTypes(childNs))
            {
                yield return type;
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetNestedTypes(INamedTypeSymbol type)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in type.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return nested;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol deeper in GetNestedTypes(nested))
            {
                yield return deeper;
            }
        }
    }
}
