using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;
using Xunit;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task AllGeneratedFormatterAndResolverTypesHaveExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TestNamespace
{
    public enum MyEnum
    {
        First = 0,
        Second = 1,
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class IntKeyObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public MyEnum Kind { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(keyAsPropertyName: true)]
    public class StringKeyObject
    {
        public string Name { get; set; }
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(UnionSubA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(UnionSubB))]
    public interface IMyUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionSubA : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int ValueA { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionSubB : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string ValueB { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        var references = net80Refs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(output.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        var generatedTypes = runResult.Results
            .SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources)
            .SelectMany(gs =>
            {
                SemanticModel semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(gs.SyntaxTree);
                return gs.SyntaxTree.GetRoot()
                    .DescendantNodes()
                    .OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>()
                    .Select(decl => (Syntax: decl, Symbol: semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(decl)))
                    .Where(pair => pair.Symbol is not null);
            })
            .ToList();

        var resolver = generatedTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
            t.Symbol!.AllInterfaces.Any(i =>
                i.ContainingNamespace.ToDisplayString() == "MessagePack" &&
                i.Name == "IFormatterResolver"));
        Assert.NotNull(resolver.Symbol);
        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolver.Syntax, resolver.Symbol!, "Generated resolver");

        var intKeyFormatter = FindFormatter(generatedTypes, "TestNamespace.IntKeyObject");
        Assert.NotNull(intKeyFormatter.Symbol);
        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(intKeyFormatter.Syntax, intKeyFormatter.Symbol!, "Int-key formatter");

        var stringKeyFormatter = FindFormatter(generatedTypes, "TestNamespace.StringKeyObject");
        Assert.NotNull(stringKeyFormatter.Symbol);
        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(stringKeyFormatter.Syntax, stringKeyFormatter.Symbol!, "String-key formatter");

        var enumFormatter = FindFormatter(generatedTypes, "TestNamespace.MyEnum");
        Assert.NotNull(enumFormatter.Symbol);
        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(enumFormatter.Syntax, enumFormatter.Symbol!, "Enum formatter");

        var unionFormatter = FindFormatter(generatedTypes, "TestNamespace.IMyUnion");
        Assert.NotNull(unionFormatter.Symbol);
        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(unionFormatter.Syntax, unionFormatter.Symbol!, "Union formatter");
    }

    private static (TypeDeclarationSyntax Syntax, INamedTypeSymbol? Symbol) FindFormatter(
        IEnumerable<(TypeDeclarationSyntax Syntax, INamedTypeSymbol? Symbol)> generatedTypes,
        string targetTypeName)
    {
        return generatedTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
            t.Symbol!.AllInterfaces.Any(i =>
                i.IsGenericType &&
                i.ContainingNamespace.ToDisplayString() == "MessagePack.Formatters" &&
                i.Name == "IMessagePackFormatter" &&
                i.TypeArguments.Length == 1 &&
                i.TypeArguments[0].ToDisplayString() == targetTypeName));
    }

    private static void AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(TypeDeclarationSyntax syntax, INamedTypeSymbol symbol, string category)
    {
        bool hasAttributeSymbol = symbol.GetAttributes().Any(a =>
            a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.True(hasAttributeSymbol, $"{category} '{symbol.ToDisplayString()}' is missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");

        bool hasGlobalQualifiedSyntax = syntax.AttributeLists
            .SelectMany(al => al.Attributes)
            .Any(a => a.Name.ToString() is "global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverage" or "global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.True(hasGlobalQualifiedSyntax, $"{category} '{symbol.ToDisplayString()}' does not emit [global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
    }
}

// Copyright (c) All contributors. All rights reserved.

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2
{
    private const string TestSource = """
        using MessagePack;

        namespace TempProject
        {
            [MessagePackObject]
            public class MyObject
            {
                [Key(0)]
                public int Value { get; set; }
            }
        }
        """;

    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedFormatter_HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenNotNetStandard20OrNetFramework()
    {
        (INamedTypeSymbol formatterSymbol, string generatedText) = await CompileAndGetFormatterSymbolAsync(Array.Empty<string>());

        Assert.Contains("#if !NETSTANDARD2_0 && !NETFRAMEWORK", generatedText);
        Assert.Contains(
            formatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            attr => attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedFormatter_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenTargetingNetStandard20()
    {
        (INamedTypeSymbol formatterSymbol, _) = await CompileAndGetFormatterSymbolAsync(new[] { "NETSTANDARD2_0" });

        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            formatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            attr => attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedFormatter_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenTargetingNetFramework()
    {
        (INamedTypeSymbol formatterSymbol, _) = await CompileAndGetFormatterSymbolAsync(new[] { "NETFRAMEWORK" });

        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            formatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            attr => attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    private static async Task<(INamedTypeSymbol FormatterSymbol, string GeneratedText)> CompileAndGetFormatterSymbolAsync(string[] preprocessorSymbols)
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net8Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = net8Refs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: preprocessorSymbols);
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(TestSource, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(
            new[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() },
            parseOptions: parseOptions);
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(output.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        ClassDeclarationSyntax? formatterClass = null;
        SemanticModel? semanticModel = null;
        string? generatedText = null;

        foreach (SyntaxTree tree in output.SyntaxTrees)
        {
            SyntaxNode root = await tree.GetRootAsync();
            ClassDeclarationSyntax? candidate = root.DescendantNodes()
                .OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
                .FirstOrDefault(c => c.Identifier.Text == "MyObjectFormatter");
            if (candidate is not null)
            {
                formatterClass = candidate;
                semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(tree);
                generatedText = tree.ToString();
                break;
            }
        }

        Assert.NotNull(formatterClass);
        Assert.NotNull(semanticModel);
        Assert.NotNull(generatedText);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterSymbol = semanticModel!.GetDeclaredSymbol(formatterClass!);
        Assert.NotNull(formatterSymbol);

        return (formatterSymbol!, generatedText!);
    }
}

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task FormatterForReferencedAssemblyType_HasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80References = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        MetadataReference[] baseReferences = net80References.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack)).ToArray();
        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);

        string externalSource = """
using MessagePack;

namespace ExternalNs
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class ExternalObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        CSharpCompilation externalCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ExternalAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(externalSource, parseOptions) },
            baseReferences,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        using MemoryStream externalAssemblyStream = new MemoryStream();
        var externalEmitResult = externalCompilation.Emit(externalAssemblyStream);
        Assert.True(externalEmitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, externalEmitResult.Diagnostics));

        MetadataReference externalReference = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(externalAssemblyStream.ToArray());

        string localSource = """
using MessagePack;
using ExternalNs;

namespace LocalNs
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class LocalObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public ExternalObject External { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        CSharpCompilation localCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "LocalAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(localSource, parseOptions) },
            baseReferences.Append(externalReference),
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(localCompilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.DoesNotContain(generatorDiagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));

        INamedTypeSymbol? externalObjectSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("ExternalNs.ExternalObject");
        Assert.NotNull(externalObjectSymbol);
        Assert.Empty(externalObjectSymbol.DeclaringSyntaxReferences);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterfaceDefinition = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterfaceDefinition);
        INamedTypeSymbol targetFormatterInterface = formatterInterfaceDefinition.Construct(externalObjectSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? externalFormatterSymbol = null;
        ClassDeclarationSyntax? externalFormatterClass = null;

        foreach (SyntaxTree tree in runResult.GeneratedTrees)
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(tree);
            SyntaxNode root = await tree.GetRootAsync();
            foreach (ClassDeclarationSyntax classDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(classDecl) is INamedTypeSymbol typeSymbol &&
                    typeSymbol.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, targetFormatterInterface)))
                {
                    externalFormatterSymbol = typeSymbol;
                    externalFormatterClass = classDecl;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (externalFormatterSymbol is not null)
            {
                break;
            }
        }

        Assert.NotNull(externalFormatterSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(externalFormatterClass);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageSymbol);

        Assert.Contains(
            externalFormatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            attr => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageSymbol));

        Assert.Contains(
            "ExcludeFromCodeCoverage",
            externalFormatterClass.AttributeLists.ToFullString());

        using MemoryStream outputStream = new MemoryStream();
        var outputEmitResult = outputCompilation.Emit(outputStream);
        Assert.True(outputEmitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, outputEmitResult.Diagnostics));
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task FormatterOmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageWhenNetStandard20Defined()
    {
        await AssertExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(new[] { "NETSTANDARD2_0" }, expectAttribute: false);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task FormatterOmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageWhenNetFrameworkDefined()
    {
        await AssertExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(new[] { "NETFRAMEWORK" }, expectAttribute: false);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task FormatterIncludesExcludeFromCodeCoverageByDefault()
    {
        await AssertExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(Array.Empty<string>(), expectAttribute: true);
    }

    private static async Task AssertExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(string[] preprocessorSymbols, bool expectAttribute)
    {
        string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TestProject
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class MyObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        var parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: preprocessorSymbols);
        SyntaxTree userTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, parseOptions);

        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> netReferences = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> msgPackReferences = ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = netReferences.Concat(msgPackReferences);

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { userTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(
            new[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() },
            parseOptions: parseOptions);
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(generatorDiagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(output.GetDiagnostics(), d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeAttrSymbol = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeAttrSymbol);

        var formatterSymbols = new List<INamedTypeSymbol>();
        foreach (SyntaxTree generatedTree in output.SyntaxTrees.Where(t => t != userTree))
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(generatedTree);
            foreach (ClassDeclarationSyntax classDecl in generatedTree.GetRoot().DescendantNodes().OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                INamedTypeSymbol? symbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(classDecl);
                if (symbol is not null && symbol.AllInterfaces.Any(i => i.Name == "IMessagePackFormatter"))
                {
                    formatterSymbols.Add(symbol);
                }
            }
        }

        Assert.Contains(formatterSymbols, s => s.Name.Contains("MyObject"));

        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol formatterSymbol in formatterSymbols)
        {
            bool hasExcludeAttr = formatterSymbol.GetAttributes().Any(
                a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeAttrSymbol));
            Assert.Equal(expectAttribute, hasExcludeAttr);
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task AllGeneratedTemplateTypes_HaveExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        string testSource = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TestNamespace
{
    public enum SampleEnum
    {
        First = 0,
        Second = 1,
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(UnionImplA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(UnionImplB))]
    public interface ISampleUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplA : ISampleUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int A { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplB : ISampleUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string B { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class IntKeyModel
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public SampleEnum Kind { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public class StringKeyModel
    {
        public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = frameworkRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);
        SyntaxTree syntaxTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(testSource, parseOptions);
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { syntaxTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(runResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeAttrSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        INamedTypeSymbol? resolverInterface = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.IFormatterResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeAttrSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);
        Assert.NotNull(resolverInterface);

        INamedTypeSymbol? intKeyModelSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.IntKeyModel");
        INamedTypeSymbol? stringKeyModelSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.StringKeyModel");
        INamedTypeSymbol? sampleEnumSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.SampleEnum");
        INamedTypeSymbol? unionInterfaceSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.ISampleUnion");
        Assert.NotNull(intKeyModelSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(stringKeyModelSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(sampleEnumSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(unionInterfaceSymbol);

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> allGeneratedAssemblyTypes = GetAllTypes(outputCompilation.Assembly.GlobalNamespace).ToList();

        INamedTypeSymbol? intKeyFormatter = FindFormatterFor(allGeneratedAssemblyTypes, formatterInterface!, intKeyModelSymbol!);
        Assert.NotNull(intKeyFormatter);
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(intKeyFormatter!, excludeAttrSymbol!), $"Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {intKeyFormatter!.ToDisplayString()}");

        INamedTypeSymbol? stringKeyFormatter = FindFormatterFor(allGeneratedAssemblyTypes, formatterInterface!, stringKeyModelSymbol!);
        Assert.NotNull(stringKeyFormatter);
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(stringKeyFormatter!, excludeAttrSymbol!), $"Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {stringKeyFormatter!.ToDisplayString()}");

        INamedTypeSymbol? enumFormatter = FindFormatterFor(allGeneratedAssemblyTypes, formatterInterface!, sampleEnumSymbol!);
        Assert.NotNull(enumFormatter);
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(enumFormatter!, excludeAttrSymbol!), $"Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {enumFormatter!.ToDisplayString()}");

        INamedTypeSymbol? unionFormatter = FindFormatterFor(allGeneratedAssemblyTypes, formatterInterface!, unionInterfaceSymbol!);
        Assert.NotNull(unionFormatter);
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(unionFormatter!, excludeAttrSymbol!), $"Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {unionFormatter!.ToDisplayString()}");

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> resolvers = allGeneratedAssemblyTypes
            .Where(t => t.TypeKind == TypeKind.Class && t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, resolverInterface)))
            .ToList();
        Assert.NotEmpty(resolvers);
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol resolver in resolvers)
        {
            Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolver, excludeAttrSymbol!), $"Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverage on {resolver.ToDisplayString()}");
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetAllTypes(INamespaceSymbol ns)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in ns.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return type;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in GetNestedTypes(type))
            {
                yield return nested;
            }
        }

        foreach (INamespaceSymbol childNs in ns.GetNamespaceMembers())
        {
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in GetAllTypes(childNs))
            {
                yield return type;
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetNestedTypes(INamedTypeSymbol type)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in type.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return nested;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol deeper in GetNestedTypes(nested))
            {
                yield return deeper;
            }
        }
    }

    private static INamedTypeSymbol? FindFormatterFor(
        IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> allTypes,
        INamedTypeSymbol formatterInterface,
        INamedTypeSymbol targetType)
    {
        return allTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
            t.TypeKind == TypeKind.Class &&
            t.AllInterfaces.Any(i =>
                SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.OriginalDefinition, formatterInterface) &&
                i.TypeArguments.Length == 1 &&
                SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.TypeArguments[0], targetType)));
    }

    private static bool HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(INamedTypeSymbol symbol, INamedTypeSymbol excludeAttrSymbol)
    {
        return symbol.GetAttributes().Any(a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeAttrSymbol));
    }
}

// Copyright (c) All contributors. All rights reserved.

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Emit;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task MetadataOnlyReferencedTypeFormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        MetadataReference[] baseReferences = net80Refs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack)).ToArray();

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);

        string externalSource = """
using MessagePack;

namespace ExternalNs
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class ExternalData
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public string Name { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        CSharpCompilation externalCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ExternalAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(externalSource, parseOptions) },
            baseReferences,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        using MemoryStream externalAssemblyStream = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult emitResult = externalCompilation.Emit(externalAssemblyStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));

        MetadataReference externalReference = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(externalAssemblyStream.ToArray());

        string localSource = """
using MessagePack;
using ExternalNs;

namespace LocalNs
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class LocalData
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public ExternalData External { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        CSharpCompilation localCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "LocalAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(localSource, parseOptions) },
            baseReferences.Append(externalReference),
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(localCompilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        foreach (GeneratorRunResult result in runResult.Results)
        {
            Assert.Null(result.Exception);
        }

        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        INamedTypeSymbol externalDataSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("ExternalNs.ExternalData");
        Assert.NotNull(externalDataSymbol);
        Assert.Empty(externalDataSymbol.DeclaringSyntaxReferences);

        INamedTypeSymbol formatterInterface = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);
        INamedTypeSymbol expectedExternalFormatterInterface = formatterInterface.Construct(externalDataSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> generatedExternalFormatters = new List<INamedTypeSymbol>();
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources))
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(generatedSource.SyntaxTree);
            SyntaxNode root = generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetRoot();
            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                INamedTypeSymbol declaredSymbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl);
                if (declaredSymbol is not null &&
                    declaredSymbol.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, expectedExternalFormatterInterface)))
                {
                    generatedExternalFormatters.Add(declaredSymbol);
                }
            }
        }

        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedExternalFormatters);
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol formatterSymbol in generatedExternalFormatters)
        {
            bool hasAttribute = formatterSymbol.GetAttributes().Any(
                attr => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));
            Assert.True(
                hasAttribute,
                $"Expected generated formatter '{formatterSymbol.ToDisplayString()}' for metadata-only type '{externalDataSymbol.ToDisplayString()}' to carry [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;
using Xunit;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task AllGeneratedFormattersAndHelpersHaveExcludeFromCodeCoverage()
    {
        string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TestNamespace
{
    public enum SampleEnum
    {
        Zero = 0,
        One = 1,
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class IntKeyObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public SampleEnum Kind { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(keyAsPropertyName: true)]
    public class StringKeyObject
    {
        public string Name { get; set; }
        public int Count { get; set; }
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(UnionA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(UnionB))]
    public interface ISampleUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionA : ISampleUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int A { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionB : ISampleUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string B { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        MetadataReference[] references = frameworkRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack)).ToArray();

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() }, parseOptions: parseOptions);
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> checkedTypes = new List<INamedTypeSymbol>();
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generated in runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources))
        {
            SyntaxNode root = await generated.SyntaxTree.GetRootAsync();
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(generated.SyntaxTree);

            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (typeDecl.Members.All(m => m is BaseTypeDeclarationSyntax))
                {
                    continue;
                }

                INamedTypeSymbol? typeSymbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl);
                Assert.NotNull(typeSymbol);
                checkedTypes.Add(typeSymbol!);

                bool hasExclude = typeSymbol!.GetAttributes().Any(a =>
                    a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == typeof(ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute).FullName);

                Assert.True(
                    hasExclude,
                    $"Generated type '{typeSymbol.ToDisplayString()}' in '{generated.HintName}' is missing [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
            }
        }

        Assert.Contains(checkedTypes, t => t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => i.ToDisplayString() == "MessagePack.IFormatterResolver"));
        Assert.Contains(checkedTypes, t => t.Name == "FormatterCache");
        Assert.Contains(checkedTypes, t => t.Name.EndsWith("GetFormatterHelper", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        Assert.Contains(checkedTypes, t => ImplementsFormatterFor(t, "TestNamespace.IntKeyObject"));
        Assert.Contains(checkedTypes, t => ImplementsFormatterFor(t, "TestNamespace.StringKeyObject"));
        Assert.Contains(checkedTypes, t => ImplementsFormatterFor(t, "TestNamespace.SampleEnum"));
        Assert.Contains(checkedTypes, t => ImplementsFormatterFor(t, "TestNamespace.ISampleUnion"));
    }

    private static bool ImplementsFormatterFor(INamedTypeSymbol typeSymbol, string targetTypeFullName)
    {
        return typeSymbol.AllInterfaces.Any(i =>
            i.OriginalDefinition.ToDisplayString() == "MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter<T>" &&
            i.TypeArguments.Length == 1 &&
            i.TypeArguments[0].ToDisplayString() == targetTypeFullName);
    }
}

Defender: Ledger_2297_Objection2 · PR green · 3 passed · mutant red · 2 failed · objection covered: valid test

Defender's test
using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedResolver_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenNetStandard20Defined()
    {
        await AssertResolverExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(new[] { "NETSTANDARD2_0" }, expectAttribute: false);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedResolver_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenNetFrameworkDefined()
    {
        await AssertResolverExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(new[] { "NETFRAMEWORK" }, expectAttribute: false);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedResolver_IncludesExcludeFromCodeCoverage_ByDefault()
    {
        await AssertResolverExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(Array.Empty<string>(), expectAttribute: true);
    }

    private static async Task AssertResolverExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(string[] preprocessorSymbols, bool expectAttribute)
    {
        string source = """
            using MessagePack;

            namespace TempProject
            {
                [MessagePackObject]
                public class MyObject
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public int Value { get; set; }
                }
            }
            """;

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        MetadataReference[] references = net80Refs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack)).ToArray();

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: preprocessorSymbols);
        SyntaxTree syntaxTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, parseOptions);

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { syntaxTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(
            new[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() },
            parseOptions: parseOptions);

        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(runResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        foreach (GeneratorRunResult result in runResult.Results)
        {
            Assert.Null(result.Exception);
        }

        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        using var peStream = new MemoryStream();
        var emitResult = outputCompilation.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics.Select(d => d.ToString())));

        SyntaxTree? resolverTree = outputCompilation.SyntaxTrees.FirstOrDefault(tree =>
        {
            if (tree == syntaxTree)
            {
                return false;
            }

            string text = tree.ToString();
            return text.Contains("IFormatterResolver") && text.Contains("FormatterCache");
        });

        Assert.NotNull(resolverTree);

        SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(resolverTree!);
        ClassDeclarationSyntax[] classDeclarations = resolverTree!.GetRoot().DescendantNodes().OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>().ToArray();
        Assert.True(classDeclarations.Length >= 3, $"Expected at least 3 classes in generated resolver, but found {classDeclarations.Length}.");

        foreach (ClassDeclarationSyntax classDecl in classDeclarations)
        {
            INamedTypeSymbol? classSymbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(classDecl);
            Assert.NotNull(classSymbol);

            bool hasExcludeFromCodeCoverage = classSymbol!.GetAttributes().Any(
                attr => attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");

            if (expectAttribute)
            {
                Assert.True(hasExcludeFromCodeCoverage, $"Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute on {classSymbol.ToDisplayString()}.");
            }
            else
            {
                Assert.False(hasExcludeFromCodeCoverage, $"Did not expect ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute on {classSymbol.ToDisplayString()} when {string.Join(", ", preprocessorSymbols)} is defined.");
            }
        }
    }
}

// Copyright (c) All contributors. All rights reserved.
// Licensed under the MIT license. See LICENSE file in the project root for full license information.

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task ResolverForReferencedAssemblyType_EmitsIdenticalAttributesAndHelpersAsInSourceType()
    {
        string externalSource = """
            using MessagePack;

            namespace ExternalNamespace
            {
                [MessagePackObject]
                public struct ExternalItem
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public int Id { get; set; }
                }
            }
            """;

        string appSource = """
            using System.Collections.Generic;
            using ExternalNamespace;
            using MessagePack;

            namespace TestApp
            {
                [MessagePackObject]
                public class Holder
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public List<ExternalItem> Items { get; set; }

                    [Key(1)]
                    public ExternalItem[] Array { get; set; }

                    [Key(2)]
                    public ExternalItem? NullableItem { get; set; }
                }
            }
            """;

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> baseReferences = net80Refs.AddRange(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation externalCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ExternalLib",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(externalSource, parseOptions) },
            baseReferences,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        using MemoryStream peStream = new MemoryStream();
        var emitResult = externalCompilation.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));
        MetadataReference externalReference = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(peStream.ToArray());

        CSharpCompilation inSourceCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestApp",
            new[]
            {
                CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(externalSource, parseOptions),
                CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(appSource, parseOptions),
            },
            baseReferences,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        CSharpCompilation referencingCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestApp",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(appSource, parseOptions) },
            baseReferences.Add(externalReference),
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        ResolverInspectionResult inSourceResult = RunAndInspectResolver(inSourceCompilation, expectInSourceExternalType: true);
        ResolverInspectionResult referencingResult = RunAndInspectResolver(referencingCompilation, expectInSourceExternalType: false);

        Assert.Contains("ExternalNamespace.ExternalItem", referencingResult.GetFormatterHelperText);
        Assert.Equal(inSourceResult.ResolverName, referencingResult.ResolverName);
        Assert.Equal(inSourceResult.ResolverAttributeLists, referencingResult.ResolverAttributeLists);
        Assert.Equal(inSourceResult.FormatterCacheDeclaration, referencingResult.FormatterCacheDeclaration);
        Assert.Equal(inSourceResult.GetFormatterHelperName, referencingResult.GetFormatterHelperName);
        Assert.Equal(inSourceResult.GetFormatterHelperAttributeLists, referencingResult.GetFormatterHelperAttributeLists);
        Assert.Equal(inSourceResult.GetFormatterHelperModifiers, referencingResult.GetFormatterHelperModifiers);
    }

    private static ResolverInspectionResult RunAndInspectResolver(CSharpCompilation compilation, bool expectInSourceExternalType)
    {
        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(output.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        INamedTypeSymbol? externalItemSymbol = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("ExternalNamespace.ExternalItem");
        Assert.NotNull(externalItemSymbol);
        if (expectInSourceExternalType)
        {
            Assert.NotEmpty(externalItemSymbol!.DeclaringSyntaxReferences);
        }
        else
        {
            Assert.Empty(externalItemSymbol!.DeclaringSyntaxReferences);
        }

        GeneratedSourceResult resolverSource = runResult.Results
            .SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources)
            .Single(gs => gs.SyntaxTree.GetRoot().DescendantNodes()
                .OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
                .Any(c => c.Identifier.ValueText == "FormatterCache"));

        SyntaxTree resolverTree = output.SyntaxTrees.Single(t => t.FilePath == resolverSource.SyntaxTree.FilePath);
        SemanticModel semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(resolverTree);
        SyntaxNode root = resolverTree.GetRoot();

        ClassDeclarationSyntax formatterCacheSyntax = root.DescendantNodes()
            .OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
            .Single(c => c.Identifier.ValueText == "FormatterCache");
        ClassDeclarationSyntax resolverSyntax = Assert.IsType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>(formatterCacheSyntax.Parent);
        ClassDeclarationSyntax getFormatterHelperSyntax = resolverSyntax.Members
            .OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
            .Single(c => c.Identifier.ValueText.EndsWith("GetFormatterHelper", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        INamedTypeSymbol resolverSymbol = Assert.IsAssignableFrom<INamedTypeSymbol>(semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(resolverSyntax));
        INamedTypeSymbol formatterCacheSymbol = Assert.IsAssignableFrom<INamedTypeSymbol>(semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(formatterCacheSyntax));
        INamedTypeSymbol getFormatterHelperSymbol = Assert.IsAssignableFrom<INamedTypeSymbol>(semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(getFormatterHelperSyntax));

        Assert.Contains(resolverSymbol.GetAttributes(), a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));
        Assert.Contains(formatterCacheSymbol.GetAttributes(), a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));
        Assert.Contains(getFormatterHelperSymbol.GetAttributes(), a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));

        return new ResolverInspectionResult(
            resolverSyntax.Identifier.ValueText,
            NormalizeLineEndings(resolverSyntax.AttributeLists.ToFullString()),
            NormalizeLineEndings(formatterCacheSyntax.ToFullString()),
            getFormatterHelperSyntax.Identifier.ValueText,
            NormalizeLineEndings(getFormatterHelperSyntax.AttributeLists.ToFullString()),
            getFormatterHelperSyntax.Modifiers.ToString(),
            getFormatterHelperSyntax.ToString());
    }

    private static string NormalizeLineEndings(string text)
        => text.Replace("\r\n", "\n", StringComparison.Ordinal).Trim();

    private sealed record ResolverInspectionResult(
        string ResolverName,
        string ResolverAttributeLists,
        string FormatterCacheDeclaration,
        string GetFormatterHelperName,
        string GetFormatterHelperAttributeLists,
        string GetFormatterHelperModifiers,
        string GetFormatterHelperText);
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedResolverAndFormattersHaveExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        const string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TestProject
{
    public enum SampleEnum
    {
        First = 0,
        Second = 1,
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class IntKeyObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Number { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public SampleEnum Status { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public class StringKeyObject
    {
        public int Id { get; set; }

        public string Label { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(UnionImplA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(UnionImplB))]
    public interface ISampleUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplA : ISampleUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionImplB : ISampleUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> msgPackRefs = ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack);
        MetadataReference[] references = net80Refs.Concat(msgPackRefs).ToArray();

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.All(runResult.Results, result => Assert.Null(result.Exception));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(generatorDiagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics(), d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);

        ImmutableArray<GeneratedSourceResult> generatedSources = runResult.Results
            .SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources)
            .ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedSources);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeAttrSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeAttrSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? resolverInterface = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.IFormatterResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(resolverInterface);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> generatedTypes = new();
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in generatedSources)
        {
            SyntaxTree tree = outputCompilation.SyntaxTrees.First(t => t.FilePath == generatedSource.SyntaxTree.FilePath);
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(tree);
            SyntaxNode root = await tree.GetRootAsync();
            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl) is INamedTypeSymbol typeSymbol)
                {
                    generatedTypes.Add(typeSymbol);
                }
            }
        }

        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedTypes);

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> resolverTypes = generatedTypes
            .Where(t => t.AllInterfaces.Contains(resolverInterface, SymbolEqualityComparer.Default))
            .ToList();
        Assert.NotEmpty(resolverTypes);

        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol resolverType in resolverTypes)
        {
            Assert.True(
                HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolverType, excludeAttrSymbol!),
                $"Generated resolver '{resolverType.ToDisplayString()}' is missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");

            ImmutableArray<INamedTypeSymbol> nestedTypes = resolverType.GetTypeMembers();
            Assert.NotEmpty(nestedTypes);
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nestedType in nestedTypes)
            {
                Assert.True(
                    HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(nestedType, excludeAttrSymbol!),
                    $"Generated resolver nested type '{nestedType.ToDisplayString()}' is missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
            }
        }

        string[] targetMetadataNames =
        {
            "TestProject.IntKeyObject",
            "TestProject.StringKeyObject",
            "TestProject.SampleEnum",
            "TestProject.ISampleUnion",
        };

        foreach (string targetMetadataName in targetMetadataNames)
        {
            INamedTypeSymbol? targetSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName(targetMetadataName);
            Assert.NotNull(targetSymbol);

            INamedTypeSymbol constructedFormatterInterface = formatterInterface!.Construct(targetSymbol!);
            INamedTypeSymbol? formatterType = generatedTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
                t.AllInterfaces.Contains(constructedFormatterInterface, SymbolEqualityComparer.Default));
            Assert.NotNull(formatterType);
            Assert.True(
                HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(formatterType!, excludeAttrSymbol!),
                $"Generated formatter '{formatterType!.ToDisplayString()}' for '{targetMetadataName}' is missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        }

        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol generatedType in generatedTypes)
        {
            Assert.True(
                HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(generatedType, excludeAttrSymbol!),
                $"Generated type '{generatedType.ToDisplayString()}' is missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        }
    }

    private static bool HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(INamedTypeSymbol typeSymbol, INamedTypeSymbol excludeAttrSymbol)
    {
        return typeSymbol.GetAttributes().Any(attr =>
            SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeAttrSymbol));
    }
}

using System;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Emit;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedResolver_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenNetStandard20Defined()
    {
        await RunGeneratorAndVerifyResolverAsync(expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage: false, "NETSTANDARD2_0");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedResolver_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenNetFrameworkDefined()
    {
        await RunGeneratorAndVerifyResolverAsync(expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage: false, "NETFRAMEWORK");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedResolver_IncludesExcludeFromCodeCoverage_ByDefault()
    {
        await RunGeneratorAndVerifyResolverAsync(expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage: true);
    }

    private static async Task RunGeneratorAndVerifyResolverAsync(bool expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage, params string[] preprocessorSymbols)
    {
        const string source = """
            using MessagePack;

            namespace TestNamespace
            {
                [MessagePackObject]
                public class SampleObject
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public int Id { get; set; }

                    [Key(1)]
                    public string Name { get; set; }
                }
            }
            """;

        var frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        var references = frameworkRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: preprocessorSymbols);
        SyntaxTree syntaxTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, parseOptions);

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { syntaxTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(
            new ISourceGenerator[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() },
            parseOptions: parseOptions);

        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));

        using MemoryStream peStream = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult emitResult = outputCompilation.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error)));

        INamedTypeSymbol resolverType = outputCompilation.Assembly.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.GeneratedMessagePackResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(resolverType);

        INamedTypeSymbol formatterCacheType = Assert.Single(resolverType.GetTypeMembers("FormatterCache"));
        INamedTypeSymbol helperType = Assert.Single(resolverType.GetTypeMembers("GeneratedMessagePackResolverGetFormatterHelper"));

        const string excludeAttrFullName = "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute";

        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol targetType in new[] { resolverType, formatterCacheType, helperType })
        {
            bool hasExcludeAttr = targetType.GetAttributes().Any(
                a => string.Equals(a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString(), excludeAttrFullName, StringComparison.Ordinal));

            if (expectExcludeFromCodeCoverage)
            {
                Assert.True(hasExcludeAttr, $"Expected {targetType.Name} to have [{excludeAttrFullName}].");
            }
            else
            {
                Assert.False(hasExcludeAttr, $"Expected {targetType.Name} NOT to have [{excludeAttrFullName}] when {string.Join(", ", preprocessorSymbols)} is defined.");
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator.Analyzers;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Diagnostics;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Emit;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task GeneratedCodeWithExcludeFromCodeCoverage_ProducesNoNewCompilerOrAnalyzerDiagnostics()
    {
        const string testSource = @"
using MessagePack;

namespace TempProject
{
    [Union(0, typeof(DerivedA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(DerivedB))]
    public interface IBaseUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class DerivedA : IBaseUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class DerivedB : IBaseUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string Name { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public class StringKeyedObject
    {
        public int Count { get; set; }
        public SampleEnum Kind { get; set; }
    }

    public enum SampleEnum
    {
        None = 0,
        Active = 1,
    }
}";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = frameworkRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        SyntaxTree syntaxTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(testSource, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest));
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { syntaxTree },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        ImmutableArray<DiagnosticAnalyzer> analyzers = ImmutableArray.Create<DiagnosticAnalyzer>(new MsgPack00xMessagePackAnalyzer());
        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> preGenAnalyzerDiagnostics = await compilation.WithAnalyzers(analyzers).GetAnalyzerDiagnosticsAsync();
        Diagnostic[] preGenNonHidden = (await compilation.WithAnalyzers(analyzers).GetAllDiagnosticsAsync())
            .Where(d => d.Severity != DiagnosticSeverity.Hidden)
            .ToArray();

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics);
        Assert.Empty(runResult.Diagnostics);
        Assert.Single(runResult.Results);
        Assert.Null(runResult.Results[0].Exception);
        Assert.NotEmpty(runResult.Results[0].GeneratedSources);
        Assert.Contains(
            runResult.Results[0].GeneratedSources,
            s => s.SourceText.ToString().Contains("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal)
                && s.SourceText.ToString().Contains("IFormatterResolver", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> postGenAnalyzerDiagnostics = await output.WithAnalyzers(analyzers).GetAnalyzerDiagnosticsAsync();
        Assert.Equal(
            preGenAnalyzerDiagnostics.Select(d => (d.Id, d.Severity, d.GetMessage())),
            postGenAnalyzerDiagnostics.Select(d => (d.Id, d.Severity, d.GetMessage())));
        Assert.Empty(postGenAnalyzerDiagnostics);

        Diagnostic[] postGenCompilerDiagnostics = output.GetDiagnostics()
            .Where(d => d.Severity != DiagnosticSeverity.Hidden)
            .ToArray();
        Assert.Empty(postGenCompilerDiagnostics);

        Diagnostic[] postGenNonHidden = (await output.WithAnalyzers(analyzers).GetAllDiagnosticsAsync())
            .Where(d => d.Severity != DiagnosticSeverity.Hidden)
            .ToArray();
        Assert.Equal(
            preGenNonHidden.Select(d => (d.Id, d.Severity, d.GetMessage())),
            postGenNonHidden.Select(d => (d.Id, d.Severity, d.GetMessage())));
        Assert.Empty(postGenNonHidden);

        using MemoryStream peStream = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult emitResult = output.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success);
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Emit;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;
using Xunit;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    private const string StringKeyTestSource = """
        using MessagePack;

        namespace TestProject
        {
            [MessagePackObject(true)]
            public class SampleStringKeyObject
            {
                public int Id { get; set; }
                public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
            }
        }
        """;

    [Fact]
    public async Task StringKeyFormatter_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_UnderNetStandard20()
    {
        var (formatterClass, formatterSymbol) = await GenerateAndCompileStringKeyFormatterAsync("NETSTANDARD2_0");

        Assert.Empty(formatterClass.AttributeLists
            .SelectMany(al => al.Attributes)
            .Where(a => a.Name.ToString().Contains("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal)));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(formatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task StringKeyFormatter_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_UnderNetFramework()
    {
        var (formatterClass, formatterSymbol) = await GenerateAndCompileStringKeyFormatterAsync("NETFRAMEWORK");

        Assert.Empty(formatterClass.AttributeLists
            .SelectMany(al => al.Attributes)
            .Where(a => a.Name.ToString().Contains("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal)));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(formatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task StringKeyFormatter_IncludesExcludeFromCodeCoverage_UnderModernNet()
    {
        var (formatterClass, formatterSymbol) = await GenerateAndCompileStringKeyFormatterAsync();

        Assert.Contains(formatterClass.AttributeLists.SelectMany(al => al.Attributes),
            a => a.Name.ToString().Contains("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        Assert.Contains(formatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    private static async Task<(ClassDeclarationSyntax FormatterClass, INamedTypeSymbol FormatterSymbol)> GenerateAndCompileStringKeyFormatterAsync(
        params string[] preprocessorSymbols)
    {
        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: preprocessorSymbols);
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = frameworkRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(StringKeyTestSource, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(
            new[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() },
            parseOptions: parseOptions);
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(output.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        using var peStream = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult emitResult = output.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));

        var formatterCandidates = output.SyntaxTrees
            .SelectMany(tree => tree.GetRoot().DescendantNodes().OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
                .Where(cls => cls.BaseList?.Types.Any(bt => bt.ToString().Contains("IMessagePackFormatter", StringComparison.Ordinal)) == true)
                .Select(cls => (Tree: tree, Class: cls)))
            .ToList();

        Assert.Single(formatterCandidates);
        var (formatterTree, formatterClass) = formatterCandidates[0];

        SemanticModel semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(formatterTree);
        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterSymbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(formatterClass);
        Assert.NotNull(formatterSymbol);

        return (formatterClass, formatterSymbol!);
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2

    [Fact]
    public async Task AllSiblingGeneratedArtifactsHaveExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        const string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TestNamespace
{
    public enum MyEnum
    {
        First = 0,
        Second = 1,
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class IntKeyObject
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Id { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public MyEnum Status { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public class StringKeyObject
    {
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(UnionSubA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(UnionSubB))]
    public interface IMyUnion
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionSubA : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int A { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class UnionSubB : IMyUnion
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string B { get; set; } = string.Empty;
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> references = frameworkRefs.AddRange(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(generatorDiagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(output.GetDiagnostics(), d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);

        ImmutableArray<GeneratedSourceResult> generatedSources = runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources).ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedSources);

        var generatedTypes = new List<INamedTypeSymbol>();
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in generatedSources)
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(generatedSource.SyntaxTree);
            SyntaxNode root = generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetRoot();
            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl) is INamedTypeSymbol symbol)
                {
                    generatedTypes.Add(symbol);
                }
            }
        }

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeAttrSymbol = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeAttrSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);

        INamedTypeSymbol? resolverInterface = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.IFormatterResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(resolverInterface);

        INamedTypeSymbol? intKeyType = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.IntKeyObject");
        INamedTypeSymbol? stringKeyType = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.StringKeyObject");
        INamedTypeSymbol? enumType = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.MyEnum");
        INamedTypeSymbol? unionType = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.IMyUnion");
        Assert.NotNull(intKeyType);
        Assert.NotNull(stringKeyType);
        Assert.NotNull(enumType);
        Assert.NotNull(unionType);

        INamedTypeSymbol? intKeyFormatter = FindFormatter(generatedTypes, formatterInterface!, intKeyType!);
        INamedTypeSymbol? stringKeyFormatter = FindFormatter(generatedTypes, formatterInterface!, stringKeyType!);
        INamedTypeSymbol? enumFormatter = FindFormatter(generatedTypes, formatterInterface!, enumType!);
        INamedTypeSymbol? unionFormatter = FindFormatter(generatedTypes, formatterInterface!, unionType!);
        INamedTypeSymbol? resolver = generatedTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
            t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, resolverInterface)));

        Assert.NotNull(intKeyFormatter);
        Assert.NotNull(stringKeyFormatter);
        Assert.NotNull(enumFormatter);
        Assert.NotNull(unionFormatter);
        Assert.NotNull(resolver);

        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(intKeyFormatter!, excludeAttrSymbol!);
        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(stringKeyFormatter!, excludeAttrSymbol!);
        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(enumFormatter!, excludeAttrSymbol!);
        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(unionFormatter!, excludeAttrSymbol!);
        AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolver!, excludeAttrSymbol!);
    }

    private static INamedTypeSymbol? FindFormatter(
        IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> generatedTypes,
        INamedTypeSymbol formatterInterface,
        INamedTypeSymbol targetType)
    {
        INamedTypeSymbol constructedFormatter = formatterInterface.Construct(targetType);
        return generatedTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
            t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, constructedFormatter)));
    }

    private static void AssertHasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(INamedTypeSymbol typeSymbol, INamedTypeSymbol excludeAttrSymbol)
    {
        bool hasAttribute = typeSymbol.GetAttributes().Any(attr =>
            SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeAttrSymbol));
        Assert.True(hasAttribute, $"Expected generated type '{typeSymbol.ToDisplayString()}' to have [ExcludeFromCodeCoverage].");
    }
}
