    private static async Task AssertReferencedFormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(
        string referencedSource,
        string localSource,
        string externalTypeMetadataName)
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(
            LanguageNames.CSharp,
            CancellationToken.None);
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> baseReferences = net80Refs
            .Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack))
            .ToImmutableArray();

        var parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);

        CSharpCompilation referencedCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ExternalModelsAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(referencedSource, parseOptions) },
            baseReferences,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        using var peStream = new MemoryStream();
        var emitResult = referencedCompilation.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));

        MetadataReference referencedAssemblyRef = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(peStream.ToArray());
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> localReferences = baseReferences.Add(referencedAssemblyRef);

        CSharpCompilation localCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "LocalAppAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(localSource, parseOptions) },
            localReferences,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(
            localCompilation,
            out Compilation outputCompilation,
            out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);

        Assert.DoesNotContain(generatorDiagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);

        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> outputErrors = outputCompilation
            .GetDiagnostics()
            .Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error)
            .ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.True(outputErrors.IsEmpty, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, outputErrors));

        INamedTypeSymbol? targetType = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName(externalTypeMetadataName);
        Assert.NotNull(targetType);
        Assert.Empty(targetType.DeclaringSyntaxReferences);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);

        INamedTypeSymbol expectedInterface = formatterInterface.Construct(targetType);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterType = GetAllTypes(outputCompilation.Assembly.GlobalNamespace)
            .FirstOrDefault(t => t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, expectedInterface)));

        Assert.NotNull(formatterType);
        Assert.Contains(
            formatterType.GetAttributes(),
            attr => attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");

        SyntaxReference syntaxRef = Assert.Single(formatterType.DeclaringSyntaxReferences);
        string generatedSource = (await syntaxRef.SyntaxTree.GetTextAsync()).ToString();
        Assert.Contains("[global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverage]", generatedSource, StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetAllTypes(INamespaceOrTypeSymbol container)
    {
        foreach (ISymbol member in container.GetMembers())
        {
            if (member is INamedTypeSymbol type)
            {
                yield return type;
                foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in GetAllTypes(type))
                {
                    yield return nested;
                }
            }
            else if (member is INamespaceSymbol ns)
            {
                foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in GetAllTypes(ns))
                {
                    yield return nested;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

    {

            namespace ReferencedLib
            {
                [MessagePackObject(keyAsPropertyName: true)]
                public class ReferencedStringKeyType
                {
                    public int Id { get; set; }

                    public string Name { get; set; } = string.Empty;
                }
            }
            """;

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);
        CSharpCompilation referencedCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ReferencedLib",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(referencedSource, parseOptions) },
            baseReferences,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        using MemoryStream peStream = new MemoryStream();
        var emitResult = referencedCompilation.Emit(peStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));

        MetadataReference referencedAssembly = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(peStream.ToArray());
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> mainReferences = baseReferences.Add(referencedAssembly);

        string mainSource = """
            using MessagePack;
            using ReferencedLib;

            namespace MainApp
            {
                [MessagePackObject]
                public class HostObject
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public ReferencedStringKeyType Item { get; set; }
                }
            }
            """;

            "MainApp",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(mainSource, parseOptions) },
            mainReferences,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(mainCompilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(runResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        var formatterSources = runResult.Results
            .SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources)
            .Where(s =>
            {

        ClassDeclarationSyntax formatterClassSyntax = (await outputTree.GetRootAsync())
            .DescendantNodes()
            .OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
            .Single(c => c.BaseList != null && c.BaseList.ToString().Contains("ReferencedStringKeyType"));

        INamedTypeSymbol formatterSymbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(formatterClassSyntax);
        Assert.NotNull(formatterSymbol);
        Assert.Contains(
            formatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            a => a.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

Prosecutor · nvidia/nemotron-3.5-super-vl-120b-a12b

Prosecutor 2: For a nested data type (e.g., `Outer.Inner`) or a closed generic type (e.g., `List<string>` / `Dictionary<string,int>`), is the `ExcludeFromCodeCoverage` attribute emitted inside the correct namespace/nesting scope and alongside the generic type constraints?

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task ExcludeFromCodeCoverage_IsEmittedOnNestedAndGenericStringKeyFormatters()
    {
        const string testSource = """
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using MessagePack;

namespace TempProject
{
    [MessagePackObject(keyAsPropertyName: true)]
    public class GenericStringKey<T> where T : class
    {
        public T Value { get; set; }
    }

    public partial class Outer
    {
        [MessagePackObject(keyAsPropertyName: true)]
        public class NestedStringKey
        {
            public int Id { get; set; }
        }

        [MessagePackObject]
        public class NestedGenericStringKey<TKey, TValue> where TKey : IComparable<TKey>
        {
            [Key("k")]
            public TKey Key { get; set; }

            [Key("v")]
            public TValue Value { get; set; }
        }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(keyAsPropertyName: true)]
    public class Consumer
    {
        public List<string> Items { get; set; }
        public Dictionary<string, int> Map { get; set; }
        public GenericStringKey<string> ClosedGeneric { get; set; }
        public Outer.NestedStringKey Nested { get; set; }
        public Outer.NestedGenericStringKey<string, int> NestedClosedGeneric { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> netRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> references = netRefs.AddRange(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(testSource, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        foreach (GeneratorRunResult result in runResult.Results)
        {
            Assert.Null(result.Exception);
        }

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> compileDiagnostics = outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics();
        Assert.Empty(compileDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        var generatedFormatters = new List<(INamedTypeSymbol FormatterSymbol, ITypeSymbol DataType)>();
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources))
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(generatedSource.SyntaxTree);
            SyntaxNode root = await generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetRootAsync();
            foreach (ClassDeclarationSyntax classDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                INamedTypeSymbol? symbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(classDecl);
                Assert.NotNull(symbol);

                INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = symbol!.AllInterfaces.FirstOrDefault(i =>
                    i.OriginalDefinition.ToDisplayString() == "MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter<T>");
                if (formatterInterface is not null)
                {
                    generatedFormatters.Add((symbol, formatterInterface.TypeArguments[0]));
                }
            }
        }

        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedFormatters);

        Assert.Contains(generatedFormatters, f => f.DataType.Name == "GenericStringKey" && f.FormatterSymbol.IsGenericType);
        Assert.Contains(generatedFormatters, f => f.DataType.Name == "NestedStringKey" && f.DataType.ContainingType?.Name == "Outer");
        Assert.Contains(generatedFormatters, f => f.DataType.Name == "NestedGenericStringKey" && f.DataType.ContainingType?.Name == "Outer" && f.FormatterSymbol.IsGenericType);
        Assert.Contains(generatedFormatters, f => f.DataType.Name == "Consumer");

        foreach ((INamedTypeSymbol formatterSymbol, ITypeSymbol dataType) in generatedFormatters)
        {
            bool hasExcludeFromCodeCoverage = formatterSymbol.GetAttributes().Any(attr =>
                attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
            Assert.True(
                hasExcludeFromCodeCoverage,
                $"Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute on generated formatter '{formatterSymbol.ToDisplayString()}' for '{dataType.ToDisplayString()}'.");
        }
    }
}

// Copyright (c) All contributors. All rights reserved.

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;
using Xunit;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task StringKeyFormatter_Structs_IncludeExcludeFromCodeCoverage()
    {
        string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TestProject
{
    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public struct MutableStruct
    {
        public int Id { get; set; }
        public string Name { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public readonly struct ReadOnlyStruct
    {
        public ReadOnlyStruct(int id, string name)
        {
            Id = id;
            Name = name;
        }

        public int Id { get; }
        public string Name { get; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public record struct PositionalRecordStruct(int Id, string Name);
}
""";

        await AssertFormattersHaveExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(
            source,
            "TestProject.MutableStruct",
            "TestProject.ReadOnlyStruct",
            "TestProject.PositionalRecordStruct");
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task StringKeyFormatter_FieldFirstRecordsAndClasses_IncludeExcludeFromCodeCoverage()
    {
        string source = """
using MessagePack;

namespace TestProject
{
    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public record PositionalRecord(int Id, string Name);

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public record InitOnlyRecord
    {
        public int Id { get; init; }
        public string Name { get; init; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject(true)]
    public class InitOnlyClass
    {
        public int Id { get; init; }
        public string Name { get; init; }
    }
}
""";

        await AssertFormattersHaveExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(
            source,
            "TestProject.PositionalRecord",
            "TestProject.InitOnlyRecord",
            "TestProject.InitOnlyClass");
    }

    private static async Task AssertFormattersHaveExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(string source, params string[] targetTypeMetadataNames)
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = frameworkRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        List<INamedTypeSymbol> allAssemblyTypes = GetAllTypes(outputCompilation.Assembly.GlobalNamespace).ToList();

        foreach (string metadataName in targetTypeMetadataNames)
        {
            INamedTypeSymbol? targetType = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName(metadataName);
            Assert.NotNull(targetType);

            INamedTypeSymbol? formatterType = allAssemblyTypes.SingleOrDefault(t =>
                t.AllInterfaces.Any(i =>
                    i.Name == "IMessagePackFormatter" &&
                    i.TypeArguments.Length == 1 &&
                    SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.TypeArguments[0], targetType)));

            Assert.NotNull(formatterType);

            bool hasExcludeFromCodeCoverage = formatterType.GetAttributes().Any(attr =>
                attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");

            Assert.True(
                hasExcludeFromCodeCoverage,
                $"Generated string-key formatter '{formatterType.ToDisplayString()}' for '{metadataName}' is missing ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetAllTypes(INamespaceSymbol ns)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in ns.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return type;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in GetNestedTypes(type))
            {
                yield return nested;
            }
        }

        foreach (INamespaceSymbol childNs in ns.GetNamespaceMembers())
        {
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in GetAllTypes(childNs))
            {
                yield return type;
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetNestedTypes(INamedTypeSymbol type)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in type.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return nested;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol deep in GetNestedTypes(nested))
            {
                yield return deep;
            }
        }
    }
}

Defender's test
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection1
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task AllGeneratedFormatterAndResolverTypesHaveExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute()
    {
        const string source = """
            using MessagePack;

            namespace TestNamespace
            {
                public enum MyEnum
                {
                    First = 0,
                    Second = 1,
                }

                [MessagePackObject]
                public class IntKeyObject
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public int Value { get; set; }

                    [Key(1)]
                    public MyEnum EnumValue { get; set; }
                }

                [MessagePackObject(keyAsPropertyName: true)]
                public class StringKeyObject
                {
                    public string Name { get; set; }
                }

                [Union(0, typeof(UnionImplA))]
                [Union(1, typeof(UnionImplB))]
                public interface IMyUnion
                {
                }

                [MessagePackObject]
                public class UnionImplA : IMyUnion
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public int A { get; set; }
                }

                [MessagePackObject]
                public class UnionImplB : IMyUnion
                {
                    [Key(0)]
                    public string B { get; set; }
                }
            }
            """;

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> references =
            (await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None))
            .AddRange(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest)) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out _);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));
        Assert.DoesNotContain(runResult.Diagnostics, d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);
        Assert.DoesNotContain(output.GetDiagnostics(), d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error);

        INamedTypeSymbol? iMessagePackFormatterOpen = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(iMessagePackFormatterOpen);

        INamedTypeSymbol? iFormatterResolver = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.IFormatterResolver");
        Assert.NotNull(iFormatterResolver);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        var generatedTypes = new List<INamedTypeSymbol>();
        foreach (SyntaxTree tree in runResult.GeneratedTrees)
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(tree);
            foreach (TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl in tree.GetRoot().DescendantNodes().OfType<TypeDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(typeDecl) is INamedTypeSymbol symbol)
                {
                    generatedTypes.Add(symbol);
                }
            }
        }

        INamedTypeSymbol? FindFormatterFor(string targetTypeName) =>
            generatedTypes.FirstOrDefault(t =>
                t.AllInterfaces.Any(i =>
                    SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.OriginalDefinition, iMessagePackFormatterOpen) &&
                    i.TypeArguments.Length == 1 &&
                    i.TypeArguments[0].Name == targetTypeName));

        INamedTypeSymbol? intKeyFormatter = FindFormatterFor("IntKeyObject");
        INamedTypeSymbol? stringKeyFormatter = FindFormatterFor("StringKeyObject");
        INamedTypeSymbol? enumFormatter = FindFormatterFor("MyEnum");
        INamedTypeSymbol? unionFormatter = FindFormatterFor("IMyUnion");
        List<INamedTypeSymbol> resolvers = generatedTypes
            .Where(t => t.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, iFormatterResolver)))
            .ToList();

        Assert.NotNull(intKeyFormatter);
        Assert.NotNull(stringKeyFormatter);
        Assert.NotNull(enumFormatter);
        Assert.NotNull(unionFormatter);
        Assert.NotEmpty(resolvers);

        bool HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(INamedTypeSymbol symbol) =>
            symbol.GetAttributes().Any(a =>
                SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));

        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(intKeyFormatter!), $"Expected {intKeyFormatter!.ToDisplayString()} (FormatterTemplate) to have ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(stringKeyFormatter!), $"Expected {stringKeyFormatter!.ToDisplayString()} (StringKeyFormatterTemplate) to have ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(enumFormatter!), $"Expected {enumFormatter!.ToDisplayString()} (EnumFormatterTemplate) to have ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(unionFormatter!), $"Expected {unionFormatter!.ToDisplayString()} (UnionTemplate) to have ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol resolver in resolvers)
        {
            Assert.True(HasExcludeFromCodeCoverage(resolver), $"Expected {resolver.ToDisplayString()} (ResolverTemplate) to have ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");
        }
    }
}

#nullable enable

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Emit;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection2
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task UnionFormatter_WithReferencedAssemblyMemberTypes_HasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAndCompiles()
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        MetadataReference[] allBaseRefs = frameworkRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack)).ToArray();
        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);

        string referencedSource = """
using MessagePack;

namespace ReferencedLib
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    public class ExternalMemberA
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public int Value { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class ExternalMemberB
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public string Text { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        CSharpCompilation referencedCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ReferencedLib",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(referencedSource, parseOptions) },
            allBaseRefs,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        using MemoryStream referencedStream = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult referencedEmit = referencedCompilation.Emit(referencedStream);
        Assert.True(referencedEmit.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, referencedEmit.Diagnostics));
        MetadataReference referencedAssemblyRef = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(referencedStream.ToArray());

        string mainSource = """
using MessagePack;
using ReferencedLib;

namespace TestProject
{
    [Union(0, typeof(ExternalMemberA))]
    [Union(1, typeof(ExternalMemberB))]
    public interface IMyUnion
    {
    }
}
""";

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "MainProject",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(mainSource, parseOptions) },
            allBaseRefs.Append(referencedAssemblyRef),
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation output, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(runResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Single(runResult.Results);
        Assert.Null(runResult.Results[0].Exception);

        using MemoryStream outputStream = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult outputEmit = output.Emit(outputStream);
        Assert.True(outputEmit.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, outputEmit.Diagnostics));

        INamedTypeSymbol? externalMemberA = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("ReferencedLib.ExternalMemberA");
        Assert.NotNull(externalMemberA);
        Assert.Empty(externalMemberA!.DeclaringSyntaxReferences);

        INamedTypeSymbol? unionSymbol = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestProject.IMyUnion");
        Assert.NotNull(unionSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(formatterInterface);
        INamedTypeSymbol expectedFormatterInterface = formatterInterface!.Construct(unionSymbol!);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = output.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        INamedTypeSymbol? unionFormatterType = GetAllTypes(output.Assembly.GlobalNamespace)
            .FirstOrDefault(t => t.AllInterfaces.Contains(expectedFormatterInterface, SymbolEqualityComparer.Default));

        Assert.NotNull(unionFormatterType);
        Assert.Contains(
            unionFormatterType!.GetAttributes(),
            attr => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));

        GeneratedSourceResult unionFormatterSource = runResult.Results[0].GeneratedSources
            .Single(s => s.SourceText.ToString().Contains($"class {unionFormatterType.Name}", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        Assert.Contains(
            "[global::System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverage]",
            unionFormatterSource.SourceText.ToString(),
            StringComparison.Ordinal);
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetAllTypes(INamespaceSymbol root)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in root.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return type;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in GetNestedTypes(type))
            {
                yield return nested;
            }
        }

        foreach (INamespaceSymbol childNamespace in root.GetNamespaceMembers())
        {
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol type in GetAllTypes(childNamespace))
            {
                yield return type;
            }
        }
    }

    private static IEnumerable<INamedTypeSymbol> GetNestedTypes(INamedTypeSymbol type)
    {
        foreach (INamedTypeSymbol nested in type.GetTypeMembers())
        {
            yield return nested;
            foreach (INamedTypeSymbol deeplyNested in GetNestedTypes(nested))
            {
                yield return deeplyNested;
            }
        }
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.IO;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    [Fact]
    public async Task NestedAndClosedGenericUnionFormattersCompileWithExcludeFromCodeCoverageInCorrectNesting()
    {
        string testSource = """
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using MessagePack;

namespace TestNamespace
{
    public partial class OuterContainer
    {
        [Union(0, typeof(NestedSubA))]
        [Union(1, typeof(NestedSubB))]
        public interface INestedUnion
        {
        }

        [MessagePackObject]
        public class NestedSubA : INestedUnion
        {
            [Key(0)]
            public int IntValue { get; set; }
        }

        [MessagePackObject]
        public class NestedSubB : INestedUnion
        {
            [Key(0)]
            public string StringValue { get; set; }
        }

        public partial class InnerContainer
        {
            [Union(0, typeof(DeepSubA))]
            [Union(1, typeof(DeepSubB))]
            public abstract class DeepUnionBase
            {
            }

            [MessagePackObject]
            public class DeepSubA : DeepUnionBase
            {
                [Key(0)]
                public double Value { get; set; }
            }

            [MessagePackObject]
            public class DeepSubB : DeepUnionBase
            {
                [Key(0)]
                public bool Flag { get; set; }
            }
        }
    }

    [Union(0, typeof(GenericSub<int>))]
    [Union(1, typeof(GenericSub<string>))]
    public interface IUnionWithClosedGenerics
    {
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class GenericSub<T> : IUnionWithClosedGenerics
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public T Value { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    [Union(0, typeof(Wrapper<string>))]
    [Union(1, typeof(Wrapper<int[]>))]
    [Union(2, typeof(Wrapper<IEnumerable<Guid>>))]
    public class Wrapper<T>
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public T Content { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class RootHolder
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public OuterContainer.INestedUnion NestedUnion { get; set; }

        [Key(1)]
        public OuterContainer.InnerContainer.DeepUnionBase DeepNestedUnion { get; set; }

        [Key(2)]
        public IUnionWithClosedGenerics UnionWithClosedGenerics { get; set; }

        [Key(3)]
        public Wrapper<string> ClosedWrapper { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> netRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        MetadataReference[] references = netRefs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack)).ToArray();

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "UnionExcludeFromCodeCoverageTest",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(testSource, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(runResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> compilationErrors = outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics()
            .Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error)
            .ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.True(
            compilationErrors.IsEmpty,
            "Expected generated compilation to have no errors, but found: " + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, compilationErrors.Select(d => d.ToString())));

        using MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream();
        EmitResult emitResult = outputCompilation.Emit(ms);
        Assert.True(
            emitResult.Success,
            "Expected Emit to succeed, but found: " + string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error).Select(d => d.ToString())));

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr);

        INamedTypeSymbol? iMessagePackFormatterOpen = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(iMessagePackFormatterOpen);

        INamedTypeSymbol? nestedUnionType = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.OuterContainer+INestedUnion");
        INamedTypeSymbol? deepUnionType = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.OuterContainer+InnerContainer+DeepUnionBase");
        INamedTypeSymbol? unionWithClosedGenericsType = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.IUnionWithClosedGenerics");
        INamedTypeSymbol? wrapperOpenType = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("TestNamespace.Wrapper`1");
        Assert.NotNull(nestedUnionType);
        Assert.NotNull(deepUnionType);
        Assert.NotNull(unionWithClosedGenericsType);
        Assert.NotNull(wrapperOpenType);

        SyntaxTree[] generatedTrees = runResult.Results
            .SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources)
            .Select(gs => gs.SyntaxTree)
            .ToArray();
        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedTrees);

        var formatterEntries = new List<(ClassDeclarationSyntax ClassDecl, INamedTypeSymbol FormatterSymbol, ITypeSymbol TargetType)>();

        foreach (SyntaxTree tree in generatedTrees)
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(tree);
            SyntaxNode root = await tree.GetRootAsync();
            foreach (ClassDeclarationSyntax classDecl in root.DescendantNodes().OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(classDecl) is INamedTypeSymbol symbol)
                {
                    INamedTypeSymbol? formatterInterface = symbol.AllInterfaces.FirstOrDefault(
                        i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i.OriginalDefinition, iMessagePackFormatterOpen));
                    if (formatterInterface != null && formatterInterface.TypeArguments.Length == 1)
                    {
                        formatterEntries.Add((classDecl, symbol, formatterInterface.TypeArguments[0]));
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        var nestedEntry = formatterEntries.FirstOrDefault(e => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(e.TargetType, nestedUnionType));
        Assert.NotNull(nestedEntry.FormatterSymbol);
        AssertFormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageInCorrectNesting(nestedEntry, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr!);

        var deepEntry = formatterEntries.FirstOrDefault(e => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(e.TargetType, deepUnionType));
        Assert.NotNull(deepEntry.FormatterSymbol);
        AssertFormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageInCorrectNesting(deepEntry, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr!);

        var closedGenericSubtypesEntry = formatterEntries.FirstOrDefault(e => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(e.TargetType, unionWithClosedGenericsType));
        Assert.NotNull(closedGenericSubtypesEntry.FormatterSymbol);
        AssertFormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageInCorrectNesting(closedGenericSubtypesEntry, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr!);

        var wrapperEntries = formatterEntries.Where(e => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(e.TargetType.OriginalDefinition, wrapperOpenType)).ToList();
        Assert.NotEmpty(wrapperEntries);
        foreach (var wrapperEntry in wrapperEntries)
        {
            AssertFormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageInCorrectNesting(wrapperEntry, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr!);
        }
    }

    private static void AssertFormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageInCorrectNesting(
        (ClassDeclarationSyntax ClassDecl, INamedTypeSymbol FormatterSymbol, ITypeSymbol TargetType) entry,
        INamedTypeSymbol excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr)
    {
        ClassDeclarationSyntax classDecl = entry.ClassDecl;
        INamedTypeSymbol symbol = entry.FormatterSymbol;

        Assert.Equal(Accessibility.Internal, symbol.DeclaredAccessibility);
        Assert.True(symbol.IsSealed, $"Expected formatter {symbol.ToDisplayString()} to be sealed.");

        bool hasAttribute = symbol.GetAttributes().Any(
            a => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(a.AttributeClass, excludeFromCodeCoverageAttr));
        Assert.True(hasAttribute, $"Expected formatter {symbol.ToDisplayString()} to have ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute.");

        Assert.NotNull(classDecl.Parent);
        Assert.IsNotType<CompilationUnitSyntax>(classDecl.Parent);

        int parentBodyStart;
        if (classDecl.Parent is NamespaceDeclarationSyntax nsDecl)
        {
            parentBodyStart = nsDecl.OpenBraceToken.Span.End;
        }
        else if (classDecl.Parent is FileScopedNamespaceDeclarationSyntax fsNsDecl)
        {
            parentBodyStart = fsNsDecl.SemicolonToken.Span.End;
        }
        else if (classDecl.Parent is TypeDeclarationSyntax typeDecl)
        {
            parentBodyStart = typeDecl.OpenBraceToken.Span.End;
        }
        else
        {
            Assert.Fail($"Unexpected parent syntax node for formatter {symbol.ToDisplayString()}: {classDecl.Parent.GetType().Name}");
            return;
        }

        Assert.True(
            classDecl.FullSpan.Start >= parentBodyStart,
            $"Expected formatter {symbol.ToDisplayString()} and its attributes/directives to be inside its nesting parent block.");

        string fullText = classDecl.ToFullString();
        int ifIndex = fullText.IndexOf("#if !NETSTANDARD2_0 && !NETFRAMEWORK", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        int attrIndex = fullText.IndexOf("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        int endifIndex = fullText.IndexOf("#endif", StringComparison.Ordinal);
        int classKeywordIndex = fullText.IndexOf("internal sealed class ", StringComparison.Ordinal);

        Assert.True(ifIndex >= 0, $"Expected #if guard in generated formatter:\n{fullText}");
        Assert.True(attrIndex > ifIndex, $"Expected ExcludeFromCodeCoverage after #if guard:\n{fullText}");
        Assert.True(endifIndex > attrIndex, $"Expected #endif after ExcludeFromCodeCoverage:\n{fullText}");
        Assert.True(classKeywordIndex > endifIndex, $"Expected 'internal sealed class' after #endif:\n{fullText}");
    }
}

Prosecutor 1: With NETSTANDARD2_0 or NETFRAMEWORK defined as a compilation constant, does the generated union formatter omit the ExcludeFromCodeCoverage attribute and still produce parseable C#?

The change test and invariance check run under a single target framework where neither symbol is defined, so they only exercise the attribute-present branch.     }

    [Fact]
    public async Task UnionFormatter_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenNetStandard20Defined()
    {
        (INamedTypeSymbol unionFormatterSymbol, ClassDeclarationSyntax unionFormatterSyntax) =
            await CompileAndFindUnionFormatterAsync("NETSTANDARD2_0");

        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            unionFormatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            attr => attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");

        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            unionFormatterSyntax.AttributeLists.SelectMany(list => list.Attributes),
            attr => attr.Name.ToString().Contains("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task UnionFormatter_OmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenNetFrameworkDefined()
    {
        (INamedTypeSymbol unionFormatterSymbol, ClassDeclarationSyntax unionFormatterSyntax) =
            await CompileAndFindUnionFormatterAsync("NETFRAMEWORK");

        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            unionFormatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            attr => attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");

        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            unionFormatterSyntax.AttributeLists.SelectMany(list => list.Attributes),
            attr => attr.Name.ToString().Contains("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal));
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task UnionFormatter_IncludesExcludeFromCodeCoverage_WhenNoTargetSymbolDefined()
    {
        (INamedTypeSymbol unionFormatterSymbol, _) =
            await CompileAndFindUnionFormatterAsync();

        Assert.Contains(
            unionFormatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            attr => attr.AttributeClass?.ToDisplayString() == "System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
    }

    private static async Task<(INamedTypeSymbol Symbol, ClassDeclarationSyntax Syntax)> CompileAndFindUnionFormatterAsync(
        params string[] preprocessorSymbols)
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> frameworkRefs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(
            LanguageNames.CSharp,
            CancellationToken.None);
        IEnumerable<MetadataReference> references = frameworkRefs.Concat(
            ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));

        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(
            LanguageVersion.Latest,
            preprocessorSymbols: preprocessorSymbols);

        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(UnionSource, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(
            new[] { new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator() },
            parseOptions: parseOptions);

        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(
            compilation,
            out Compilation output,
            out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);

        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.All(runResult.Results, r => Assert.Null(r.Exception));

        ImmutableArray<GeneratedSourceResult> generatedSources = runResult.Results
            .SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources)
            .ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.NotEmpty(generatedSources);

        Assert.Empty(output.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        INamedTypeSymbol? unionFormatterSymbol = null;
        ClassDeclarationSyntax? unionFormatterSyntax = null;

        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in generatedSources)
        {
            Assert.Empty(generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

            SemanticModel semanticModel = output.GetSemanticModel(generatedSource.SyntaxTree);
            foreach (ClassDeclarationSyntax classDecl in generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetRoot().DescendantNodes().OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                INamedTypeSymbol? symbol = semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(classDecl);
                if (symbol is not null && symbol.AllInterfaces.Any(i =>
                    i.Name == "IMessagePackFormatter" &&
                    i.TypeArguments.Length == 1 &&
                    i.TypeArguments[0].Name == "IMyUnion"))
                {
                    unionFormatterSymbol = symbol;
                    unionFormatterSyntax = classDecl;
                    break;
                }
            }

            if (unionFormatterSymbol is not null)
            {
                break;
            }
        }

        Assert.NotNull(unionFormatterSymbol);
        Assert.NotNull(unionFormatterSyntax);

        return (unionFormatterSymbol!, unionFormatterSyntax!);
    }
}

    private static async Task AssertExternalUnionFormatterHasExcludeFromCodeCoverageAsync(
        string externalSource,
        string consumerSource,
        string unionMetadataName)
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> baseRefs = net80Refs.AddRange(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack));
        CSharpParseOptions parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);

        CSharpCompilation externalCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ExternalUnionAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(externalSource, parseOptions) },
            baseRefs,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        using MemoryStream externalPeStream = new MemoryStream();
        var emitResult = externalCompilation.Emit(externalPeStream);
        Assert.True(emitResult.Success, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, emitResult.Diagnostics));
        MetadataReference externalRef = MetadataReference.CreateFromImage(externalPeStream.ToArray());

        CSharpCompilation consumerCompilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "ConsumerAssembly",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(consumerSource, parseOptions) },
            baseRefs.Add(externalRef),
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(consumerCompilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        foreach (GeneratorRunResult result in runResult.Results)
        {
            Assert.Null(result.Exception);
        }

        ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> outputErrors = outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error).ToImmutableArray();
        Assert.True(outputErrors.IsEmpty, string.Join(Environment.NewLine, outputErrors));

        INamedTypeSymbol? unionSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName(unionMetadataName);
        Assert.NotNull(unionSymbol);
        Assert.True(unionSymbol.DeclaringSyntaxReferences.IsEmpty);

        INamedTypeSymbol? openFormatterInterface = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("MessagePack.Formatters.IMessagePackFormatter`1");
        Assert.NotNull(openFormatterInterface);
        INamedTypeSymbol expectedFormatterInterface = openFormatterInterface.Construct(unionSymbol);

        INamedTypeSymbol? excludeAttrSymbol = outputCompilation.GetTypeByMetadataName("System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.ExcludeFromCodeCoverageAttribute");
        Assert.NotNull(excludeAttrSymbol);

        List<(INamedTypeSymbol Symbol, ClassDeclarationSyntax Syntax)> matchingFormatters = new List<(INamedTypeSymbol, ClassDeclarationSyntax)>();
        foreach (GeneratedSourceResult generatedSource in runResult.Results.SelectMany(r => r.GeneratedSources))
        {
            SemanticModel semanticModel = outputCompilation.GetSemanticModel(generatedSource.SyntaxTree);
            foreach (ClassDeclarationSyntax classDecl in generatedSource.SyntaxTree.GetRoot().DescendantNodes().OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>())
            {
                if (semanticModel.GetDeclaredSymbol(classDecl) is INamedTypeSymbol typeSymbol &&
                    typeSymbol.AllInterfaces.Any(i => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(i, expectedFormatterInterface)))
                {
                    matchingFormatters.Add((typeSymbol, classDecl));
                }
            }
        }

        (INamedTypeSymbol formatterSymbol, ClassDeclarationSyntax formatterSyntax) = Assert.Single(matchingFormatters);

        Assert.Contains(
            formatterSymbol.GetAttributes(),
            attr => SymbolEqualityComparer.Default.Equals(attr.AttributeClass, excludeAttrSymbol));

        Assert.NotNull(formatterSymbol.ContainingNamespace);
        Assert.False(formatterSymbol.ContainingNamespace.IsGlobalNamespace);
        Assert.True(
            formatterSyntax.Parent is BaseNamespaceDeclarationSyntax or TypeDeclarationSyntax,
            $"Expected formatter class to be declared inside a namespace or nesting type, but parent was {formatterSyntax.Parent?.GetType().Name}.");
    }
}

using System;
using System.Collections.Immutable;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using MessagePack.SourceGenerator;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.CSharp.Syntax;
using Microsoft.CodeAnalysis.Testing;
using Xunit;

public class Ledger_2297_Objection3
{
    private static async Task<ImmutableArray<MetadataReference>> GetReferencesAsync()
    {
        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> net80Refs = await ReferenceAssemblies.Net.Net80.ResolveAsync(LanguageNames.CSharp, CancellationToken.None);
        return net80Refs.Concat(ReferencesHelper.GetReferences(ReferencesSet.MessagePack)).ToImmutableArray();
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task UnionOverNestedClosedGenericDataType_EmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageInContextAndIsDeterministic()
    {
        string source = """
using System;
using MessagePack;

namespace TestNamespace
{
    public class Container
    {
        [Union(0, typeof(Outer<string>.Inner<int>))]
        [Union(1, typeof(Outer<long>.Inner<int>))]
        public abstract class NestedUnion
        {
        }
    }

    public class Outer<T>
    {
        [MessagePackObject]
        public class Inner<U> : Container.NestedUnion
        {
            [Key(0)]
            public T First { get; set; }

            [Key(1)]
            public U Second { get; set; }
        }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> references = await GetReferencesAsync();
        var parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly1",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        Assert.Empty(compilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(runResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Single(runResult.Results);
        Assert.Null(runResult.Results[0].Exception);
        Assert.NotEmpty(runResult.Results[0].GeneratedSources);
        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        GeneratedSourceResult unionSource = runResult.Results[0].GeneratedSources
            .Single(s => s.SourceText.ToString().Contains("typeToKeyAndJumpMap", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        string unionSourceText = unionSource.SourceText.ToString();

        SyntaxTree generatedTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(unionSourceText, parseOptions);
        ClassDeclarationSyntax formatterClass = generatedTree.GetCompilationUnitRoot()
            .DescendantNodes()
            .OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
            .Single(c => c.Members.OfType<FieldDeclarationSyntax>()
                .Any(f => f.Declaration.Variables.Any(v => v.Identifier.Text == "typeToKeyAndJumpMap")));

        Assert.Contains(formatterClass.Ancestors(), a => a is BaseNamespaceDeclarationSyntax);
        Assert.Contains(
            formatterClass.AttributeLists.SelectMany(al => al.Attributes),
            attr => attr.Name.ToString().EndsWith("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        SyntaxTree netstandardTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(
            unionSourceText,
            new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: new[] { "NETSTANDARD2_0" }));
        ClassDeclarationSyntax netstandardFormatterClass = netstandardTree.GetCompilationUnitRoot()
            .DescendantNodes()
            .OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
            .Single(c => c.Members.OfType<FieldDeclarationSyntax>()
                .Any(f => f.Declaration.Variables.Any(v => v.Identifier.Text == "typeToKeyAndJumpMap")));

        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            netstandardFormatterClass.AttributeLists.SelectMany(al => al.Attributes),
            attr => attr.Name.ToString().EndsWith("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        CSharpCompilation compilation2 = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly1",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));
        GeneratorDriver driver2 = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver2 = driver2.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation2, out _, out _);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult2 = driver2.GetRunResult();

        var generated1 = runResult.Results[0].GeneratedSources
            .OrderBy(s => s.HintName, StringComparer.Ordinal)
            .Select(s => (s.HintName, Text: s.SourceText.ToString()))
            .ToArray();
        var generated2 = runResult2.Results[0].GeneratedSources
            .OrderBy(s => s.HintName, StringComparer.Ordinal)
            .Select(s => (s.HintName, Text: s.SourceText.ToString()))
            .ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(generated1, generated2);
    }

    [Fact]
    public async Task ClosedGenericUnionType_EmitsExcludeFromCodeCoverageInContextAndIsDeterministic()
    {
        string source = """
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using MessagePack;

namespace TestNamespace
{
    [MessagePackObject]
    [Union(0, typeof(Wrapper<string>))]
    [Union(1, typeof(Wrapper<int[]>))]
    [Union(2, typeof(Wrapper<IEnumerable<Guid>>))]
    public class Wrapper<T>
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public T Content { get; set; }
    }

    [MessagePackObject]
    public class Holder
    {
        [Key(0)]
        public Wrapper<string> ClosedWrapper { get; set; }
    }
}
""";

        ImmutableArray<MetadataReference> references = await GetReferencesAsync();
        var parseOptions = new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest);
        CSharpCompilation compilation = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly2",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));

        Assert.Empty(compilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        GeneratorDriver driver = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver = driver.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation, out Compilation outputCompilation, out ImmutableArray<Diagnostic> generatorDiagnostics);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult = driver.GetRunResult();

        Assert.Empty(generatorDiagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Empty(runResult.Diagnostics.Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));
        Assert.Single(runResult.Results);
        Assert.Null(runResult.Results[0].Exception);
        Assert.NotEmpty(runResult.Results[0].GeneratedSources);
        Assert.Empty(outputCompilation.GetDiagnostics().Where(d => d.Severity == DiagnosticSeverity.Error));

        GeneratedSourceResult wrapperSource = runResult.Results[0].GeneratedSources
            .Single(s => s.SourceText.ToString().Contains("WrapperFormatter", StringComparison.Ordinal));
        string wrapperSourceText = wrapperSource.SourceText.ToString();

        SyntaxTree generatedTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(wrapperSourceText, parseOptions);
        ClassDeclarationSyntax formatterClass = generatedTree.GetCompilationUnitRoot()
            .DescendantNodes()
            .OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
            .Single(c => c.Identifier.Text.StartsWith("WrapperFormatter", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        Assert.Contains(formatterClass.Ancestors(), a => a is BaseNamespaceDeclarationSyntax);
        Assert.Contains(
            formatterClass.AttributeLists.SelectMany(al => al.Attributes),
            attr => attr.Name.ToString().EndsWith("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        SyntaxTree netframeworkTree = CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(
            wrapperSourceText,
            new CSharpParseOptions(LanguageVersion.Latest, preprocessorSymbols: new[] { "NETFRAMEWORK" }));
        ClassDeclarationSyntax netframeworkFormatterClass = netframeworkTree.GetCompilationUnitRoot()
            .DescendantNodes()
            .OfType<ClassDeclarationSyntax>()
            .Single(c => c.Identifier.Text.StartsWith("WrapperFormatter", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        Assert.DoesNotContain(
            netframeworkFormatterClass.AttributeLists.SelectMany(al => al.Attributes),
            attr => attr.Name.ToString().EndsWith("ExcludeFromCodeCoverage", StringComparison.Ordinal));

        CSharpCompilation compilation2 = CSharpCompilation.Create(
            "TestAssembly2",
            new[] { CSharpSyntaxTree.ParseText(source, parseOptions) },
            references,
            new CSharpCompilationOptions(OutputKind.DynamicallyLinkedLibrary));
        GeneratorDriver driver2 = CSharpGeneratorDriver.Create(new MessagePackGenerator().AsSourceGenerator());
        driver2 = driver2.RunGeneratorsAndUpdateCompilation(compilation2, out _, out _);
        GeneratorDriverRunResult runResult2 = driver2.GetRunResult();

        var generated1 = runResult.Results[0].GeneratedSources
            .OrderBy(s => s.HintName, StringComparer.Ordinal)
            .Select(s => (s.HintName, Text: s.SourceText.ToString()))
            .ToArray();
        var generated2 = runResult2.Results[0].GeneratedSources
            .OrderBy(s => s.HintName, StringComparer.Ordinal)
            .Select(s => (s.HintName, Text: s.SourceText.ToString()))
            .ToArray();

        Assert.Equal(generated1, generated2);
    }
}
